We evaluate AI control for automated AI R&D with ResearchArena, a framework spanning four long-horizon tasks: safety post-training, capabilities post-training, CUDA-kernel optimization, and inference-server optimization. Because the deliverable in AI R&D is an artifact that will be deployed and run, we pair each main task with two kinds of hidden side task: an embedded side task that sabotages the submitted model, adapter, kernel, or server itself, and an independent side task that takes a covert out-of-scope action in the sandbox while leaving the artifact honest. We evaluate frontier agents at both sabotage and monitoring, across four monitor types that vary along two axes: whether the monitor may execute and probe the produced artifact, and whether it sees the agent's chain-of-thought. We find that sabotage hidden in the training data is the hardest to catch, flagged fewer than half the time. Letting the monitor run experiments on the artifact, rather than only read the trajectory, helps, but it is not enough: monitors still miss embedded sabotage by inspecting only the surface, by noticing the anomaly but explaining it away, or by probing the artifact with the wrong test. We release ResearchArena as a modular framework for evaluating sabotage and control in automated AI R&D.

</details>


### [72] [Associative Emotional Learning in Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19327)
*Seowung Leem,Andreas Keil,Mingzhou Ding,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个深度神经网络模型来模拟视觉效价加工中的联想情绪学习，通过巴甫洛夫学习范式在模型上重现了人类联想学习中的多个行为与神经表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算模型（如Rescorla-Wagner模型）在解释联想情绪学习的神经数据方面存在局限，需要更强大的建模工具来理解这一过程。

Method: 构建了一个深度神经网络模型，包含视觉模块（编码复杂自然场景）和情绪识别模块（识别效价维度），并在该模型上测试了新的巴甫洛夫学习范式。

Result: 模型成功再现了人类联想学习中的联结形成与泛化现象，条件刺激与非条件刺激的神经表征在单单元和群体水平上逐渐对齐，且与人类实验数据对比验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 将深度神经网络与适当的学习算法相结合，可以有效建模联想情绪/效价学习的行为与神经特征。

Abstract: Associative emotional learning enables organisms to adaptively link pleasant or unpleasant outcomes to the presence of predictive stimuli. Whereas computational models such as the Rescorla-Wagner model have shed light on this important function, the limitations of these models are also known, especially when they are applied to neural data. The advent of deep neural networks has opened another avenue for modeling associative emotional learning. In this work we proposed a deep neural network model of visual valence processing, consisting of a visual module that encodes complex natural scenes and a module that recognizes their emotional significance in terms of valence, a key dimension of emotion, and tested a novel Pavlovian learning paradigm on the model. The results showed that with learning, the model reproduced several observations from human associative learning studies, including association formation and generalization, and that the neural representations of the conditioned and the unconditioned stimuli became increasingly aligned both at the single unit and at the neural population level. Comparison between the model and human experimental data provided further validation of our approach. This study thus suggests that deep neural network models, when combined with appropriate learning algorithms, can be used to model behavioral and neural signatures of associative emotion/valence learning.

</details>


### [73] [Agents in the Wild: Where Research Meets Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19336)
*Grace Hui Yang,Pranav N. Venkit,Hooman Sedghamiz,Enrico Santus,Victor Dibia,Ioana Baldini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本教程系统探讨了基于大语言模型的智能体系统从研究原型到生产部署的进展，涵盖推理规划、多智能体协调、评估等挑战，并通过制药发现和金融系统的案例研究，分析了成功的设计模式及失败缓解策略（如验证管线、回退机制、人在回路监督），为安全可靠的跨行业部署提供了设计模式、评估检查清单和模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统正从研究原型快速转向生产规模部署，但在鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性方面面临新挑战，现有学术工作侧重于基准和算法创新，缺乏对部署实践问题的系统指导。

Method: 通过汇集研究人员和实践者，结合制药发现和金融系统等应用案例，分析常见设计模式，讨论验证管线、回退机制、人在回路监督等失败缓解策略。

Result: 提供了智能体系统成功的设计模式、评估检查清单和安全可靠部署的模板。

Conclusion: 参与者将获得对该领域的全面理解，以及跨行业安全可靠部署的具体设计模式、评估检查清单和模板。

Abstract: Agentic systems large language model (LLM) based architectures capable of reasoning, planning, acting, and coordinating with tools and other agents are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to production scale deployments across domains such as software engineering, scientific discovery, and finance. While academic work has emphasized benchmarks and algorithmic innovation, deployment raises new challenges around robustness, safety, and reliability. This tutorial brings together researchers and practitioners to explore advances in reasoning and planning, multi agent coordination, and evaluation, highlighting open challenges arising from deployment experience. Through applied case studies in pharmaceutical discovery and financial systems, we analyze common design patterns that make agentic systems successful, and discuss practical mitigation strategies for failure modes, such as verification pipelines, fallback mechanisms, and human in the loop supervision. Attendees will gain a comprehensive view of the field along with concrete design patterns, evaluation checklists, and templates for safe and reliable deployment across industries.

</details>


### [74] [CodeRescue: Budget-Calibrated Recovery Routing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19338)
*Qijia He,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Rui Wang,Xunmei Liu,Yixian Chen,Jie Mei,Zhihao Wang,Xupeng Chen,Yuhuan Chen,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向代码智能体的预算控制方法，在智能体执行失败后，通过监督学习训练恢复路由（recovery router）在廉价重试与昂贵升级之间做决策，并引入保形风险控制（CRC）层，使同一路由器无需重新训练即可适应不同预算约束。实验表明，该方法在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设置下，以35%的平均恢复成本达到或超过始终升级的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本感知系统通常采用级联策略（先用廉价模型，失败后升级到强模型），但在代码执行场景中，执行失败后的反馈信息也可能使廉价模型的进一步重试产生价值。因此需要回答一个预算约束下的部署问题：智能体何时应该花费更多廉价计算资源进行恢复，何时应该升级到更强模型？

Method: 1) 将失败后的决策建模为异构动作上的恢复路由问题（recovery routing），包括廉价重试和昂贵升级两种动作；2) 从执行轨迹中收集数据，训练一个监督路由器；3) 引入保形风险控制（Conformal Risk Control, CRC）层，在不重新训练模型的情况下调整部署时的成本惩罚项，实现对期望成本的可控边界，且保证在可交换性条件下的边际期望成本控制。

Result: 在五个编码基准测试的失败样本上，廉价恢复和升级策略表现出互补的成功模式。校准后的前沿（calibrated frontier）优于固定动作、仅提示路由器和二元级联基线。在主要GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4对比设置中，一个CRC校准的前沿点以35%的平均恢复成本达到或超过始终升级的解决率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的路由+CRC框架能有效在代码智能体的恢复与升级之间做出成本感知的决策，在显著降低推理成本的同时不牺牲性能，且具备跨预算场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly operate in executable environments where a failed attempt produces actionable feedback rather than merely an incorrect answer. Existing cost-aware systems typically treat such failures as cascade decisions: try a cheap model first, then escalate hard cases to a stronger and more expensive model. In coding, however, execution feedback can also make further cheap-model recovery worthwhile, raising a budgeted deployment question: when should an agent spend more cheap compute, and when should it escalate? We formulate this post-failure decision as recovery routing over heterogeneous actions and train a supervised router from execution rollouts. To make the same router usable under changing budgets, we add a Conformal Risk Control (CRC) layer that selects a deployment-time cost penalty without retraining and provides marginal expected-cost control under exchangeability. Across held-out failures from five coding benchmarks, cheap recovery and escalation exhibit complementary success patterns. The calibrated frontier improves over fixed actions, prompt-only routers, and a binary cascade baseline; in the main GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4 setting, one CRC-calibrated frontier point exceeds always-escalate solve rate while using 35% of its mean recovery cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Qijia-He/agent-budget-control.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [FALCON-Discover: Discovering Concentrated False-Confidence Regions for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18278)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FALCON-Discover框架，通过置信度、局部支持、邻居一致性和扰动稳定性等差异信号来发现模型预测中高置信度但错误（假自信集中）的区域。实验表明，这种基于差异的排序方法在多个数据集和强学习器上显著优于传统校准方法，且最佳检测器因数据集而异，说明危险过置信更适合作为发现问题而非校准问题处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统校准评估关注整体聚合指标，但最危险的失败往往是局部的——模型以高置信度做出错误预测。这种假自信集中现象在关键领域（如医疗、金融）可能造成严重后果，因此需要能够定位这些危险区域的方法。

Method: 提出FALCON-Discover框架，一种后处理、模型无关的方法。该方法利用置信度、局部支持、邻居一致性和扰动稳定性四种差异信号对预测进行排序，以发现假自信集中区域。在7个二分类表格数据集、4个随机种子、5折交叉拟合上使用XGBoost和CatBoost等强学习器进行验证。

Result: 假自信集中现象具有重复性但依赖于特定场景。在主置信度阈值下，基于差异的排序在最强场景中显著优于验证集选取的最佳校准或信任评分基线，而原始置信度几乎无法捕获危险错误。最佳检测器因数据集而异：当需要组合多个线索时，学习到的差异最强；当局部决策脆弱性占主导时，稳定性中心的排序效果最好。

Conclusion: 危险过置信更适合作为族级发现问题而非单分数校准问题处理，应激励校准策略明确针对置信度、支持度和稳定性出现分歧的区域。

Abstract: Calibration is usually evaluated in aggregate, but the most dangerous failures are often local: predictions that remain highly confident despite being wrong. We study this failure mode as false-confidence concentration, the extent to which confident errors occupy compact, discoverable regions of prediction space. We introduce FALCON-Discover, a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework that ranks predictions using discrepancy signals from confidence, local support, neighborhood agreement, and perturbation stability. Across seven binary tabular datasets, four seeds, five-fold cross-fitting, and strong learners including XGBoost and CatBoost, we find that false-confidence concentration is recurrent but regime-dependent. At the main confidence threshold, discrepancy-based ranking substantially outperforms the strongest validation-selected calibration or trust-scoring baseline in the strongest regimes, while raw confidence recovers little dangerous-error mass. The best detector varies across datasets: learned discrepancy is strongest when multiple cues must be combined, whereas stability-centered ranking works best when local decisional fragility dominates. These results show that dangerous overconfidence is better treated as a family-level discovery problem than as a single-score calibration problem, and motivate calibration strategies that explicitly target regions where confidence, support, and stability diverge.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Output-Space Calibration: Spectral Evidence Bundling for Selective Reliability Estimation in Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18279)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对时间序列分类的可信度估计问题，提出一种验证门控固定标签可靠性策略，结合输出置信度与频谱特征（如频带能量、熵值、峰值优势、周期支持、相位稳定性）来估计预测是否可信，并通过验证门控确保仅在正确率排序提升且不违反错误高置信度或AURC容差时才启用频谱调节，否则回退到输出空间基线。在8个数据集、8种骨干网络和标准重校准器上，该方法将Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779（无约束）和0.786（门控策略），并将FalseConf@0.9降至0.094。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后校准通常只重映射输出分数，但部署决策（信任、弃权、审查）取决于当前时间信号是否支持高置信预测。现有方法存在三个时间序列可靠性缺口：相同置信度可能隐藏不同时间支持、平均校准可能遗漏假高置信错误、输出空间重校准提供的输入关联审计性有限。

Method: 提出验证门控固定标签可靠性策略，保持骨干预测不变，混合输出端线索与全样本频谱描述子（频带能量、熵、峰值优势、周期支持、相位稳定性）形成标量可靠性估计和诊断级频带证据。验证门控仅在正确率排序提升且不违反FalseConf@0.9或AURC容差时启用频谱调节，否则回退到输出空间基线。

Result: 在8个UCR/UEA数据集、8种时间序列骨干网络和标准重校准器上，无约束方法将Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779；验证门控策略进一步将Corr-AURC提升至0.786，并将FalseConf@0.9降至0.094。

Conclusion: 时间序列分类器的可靠性估计受益于将输出置信度与频谱证据捆绑，而验证门控可防止无支持的频谱调节导致性能下降。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration for time-series classification usually remaps output scores, but deployment decisions such as trust, abstention, and review depend on whether a confident prediction is supported by the current temporal signal. We address three time-series reliability gaps: identical confidence values can hide different temporal support, average calibration can miss false high-confidence errors, and output-space recalibration offers limited input-linked auditability. We introduce a validation-gated fixed-label reliability policy that keeps the backbone prediction unchanged while estimating whether it should be trusted. The method combines output-side cues with whole-sample spectral descriptors, including band energy, entropy, peak dominance, period support, and phase stability, to form a scalar reliability estimate and diagnostic band-level evidence. A validation gate enables spectral conditioning only when correctness ranking improves without breaching FalseConf@0.9 or AURC tolerances; otherwise it reverts to the safer output-space baseline. Across eight heterogeneous UCR/UEA datasets, eight time-series backbone families, and standard recalibrators, the unconstrained method improves fixed-label selective-reliability metrics on the matched evaluation subset, raising Corr-AURC from 0.693 to 0.779. The validation-gated policy further improves Corr-AURC to 0.786 and reduces FalseConf@0.9 to 0.094. These results suggest that reliability estimation for time-series classifiers benefits from bundling output confidence with spectral evidence, while validation gating prevents unsupported spectral conditioning.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Single-Dimensional Compression: The Compound Sparsity Frontier of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18280)
*Chao Han,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合低秩近似、通道剪枝和动态层跳过的复合稀疏化框架，通过将参数稀疏和token级计算稀疏相结合，在相同总稀疏度下优于单一压缩机制，延缓了性能退化点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM压缩方法（静态参数剪枝或动态token级计算）在超过必要稀疏边界时会导致性能急剧下降，本文探索是否可以通过组合这两种机制来分担压缩负担，从而延缓这种退化。

Method: 采用两阶段复合稀疏框架：首先通过低秩近似和通道剪枝获得静态压缩主干网络，然后引入轻量级路由器实现逐token的动态层跳过，从而独立控制参数稀疏度和token级计算稀疏度。

Result: 复合稀疏在相同总稀疏度下始终优于单一压缩机制，在理解任务上延缓了性能衰减点，并保持了更强的建模性能；近平衡的稀疏分配在固定预算下最有效。

Conclusion: 复合压缩是提升LLM压缩效果的实用方法，揭示了参数剪枝与token跳过之间的跨维度干扰，以及最终限制进一步压缩的跨维度稀疏边界。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compressed through static parameter pruning or dynamic token-level computation, yet aggressive sparsification can trigger rapid performance degradation beyond an essential sparsity boundary. This work asks \emph{whether combining these two mechanisms can delay such degradation by distributing the compression burden}. We study a minimalist compound sparsity framework that first applies low-rank approximation and channel pruning to obtain a statically compressed backbone, and then introduces lightweight routers for per-token dynamic layer skipping. This design enables independent control of parameter sparsity and token-level computation sparsity. Experiments across language understanding and modeling benchmarks show that compound sparsity consistently outperforms single-mechanism compression under the same total sparsity, delaying the decay point on understanding tasks and preserving stronger modeling performance. Further analysis reveals cross-dimensional interference between parameter pruning and token skipping, and shows that near-balanced allocation is most effective under a fixed sparsity budget. These results demonstrate that compound compression provides a practical way to improve LLM compression, while revealing a broader cross-dimensional sparsity boundary that ultimately limits further compression. Code will be available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning.

</details>


### [78] [ALAS: Additive Learnable Alpha-Stable Kernels for Flexible Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18282)
*Weibo Huang,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ALAS提出了一种基于对称α稳定谱成分的高斯过程核函数族，通过学习稳定性参数α自适应调整平滑度，能够同时捕捉平滑趋势和尖锐不规则性，在各类优化任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化的成功常依赖于选择与目标函数未知结构匹配的核函数，现有方法缺乏灵活性，难以同时适应平滑和剧烈变化的目标。

Method: 提出ALAS核函数族，基于对称α稳定谱成分；包含ALAS（联合谱调制的单平稳成分）和ALAS-Sep（可分离变体，逐维学习尾行为）两种参数化形式。

Result: 在标准基准测试和真实代理模型上，ALAS在不同设置下均展现出强且鲁棒的优化性能。

Conclusion: ALAS通过自适应学习稳定性参数α，有效解决了核函数选择难题，在多样化的优化场景中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is widely used for expensive black-box optimization, yet its success often depends on choosing a kernel that matches the objective's unknown structure. In this work, we propose ALAS, a flexible Gaussian Process kernel family built from symmetric $α$-stable spectral components. By learning the stability parameter $α$, ALAS adapts its effective smoothness from data, capturing both smooth trends and sharp irregularities. We present two parameterizations: ALAS, a single stationary component with joint spectral modulation, and ALAS-Sep, a separable variant that learns dimension-wise tail behavior to improve robustness on approximately decomposable objectives. Experiments on standard benchmarks and real-world surrogates demonstrate strong and robust performance across diverse settings.

</details>


### [79] [FedCC: A Low-Resource Federated Adaptation of Foundation Models for Robust Corpus Callosum localization in Fetal Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18283)
*Alessandro Di Matteo,Sara Moccia,Giuseppe Rizzo,Gianpaolo Grisolia,Ricciarda Raffaelli,Lorenzo Vasciaveo,Francesco D'Antonio,Maria Chiara Fiorentino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCC，一种基于联邦学习的框架，用于在胎儿超声图像中定位胼胝体。该方法结合冻结的DINOv2骨干网络、轻量级YOLO检测头和LoRA模块，在10,970帧多中心数据集上实现mAP@50为0.857，仅需2.9M可训练参数，通信成本降低约8.5倍，兼顾了隐私保护、资源效率和高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿超声图像中胼胝体准确定位对早期识别神经发育异常至关重要，但面临超声成像对比度低、散斑噪声、解剖变异大等挑战。现有方法难以在多中心、资源受限且需保护数据隐私的临床场景中有效部署。

Method: 提出FedCC联邦学习框架，集成冻结的DINOv2作为视觉骨干网络，搭配轻量级YOLO检测头。引入LoRA模块实现参数高效适配，仅优化和交换少量参数（2.9M），采用FedAvg聚合策略进行联邦训练。

Result: 在来自三个临床站点、58名孕妇、10,970帧超声图像的多中心数据集上，FedCC结合DINOv2和LoRA及FedAvg策略达到平均mAP@50为0.857，F1-score为0.803。可训练参数从全微调的24.4M降至2.9M，通信成本降低约8.5倍。

Conclusion: FedCC为可扩展、隐私保护、临床可部署的胎儿神经超声AI系统迈出了有希望的一步，实现了在资源受限场景下高效且准确的胼胝体定位。

Abstract: Accurate localization of the corpus callosum (CC) in fetal ultrasound (US) images is crucial for the early identification of neurodevelopmental abnormalities. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intrinsic limitations of US imaging, including low contrast, speckle noise, and the considerable anatomical variability of the CC. We propose FedCC, a federated learning (FL)-based framework for CC localization in fetal US images, specifically designed for realistic multi-center and resource-constrained clinical settings without requiring data sharing. The framework integrates a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a lightweight YOLO-based detection head. To enable parameter-efficient adaptation, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are incorporated, allowing only a small subset of parameters to be optimized and exchanged among clients. This strategy substantially reduces both computational and communication overhead, making the framework suitable for low-resource environments. The proposed approach was evaluated on a multi-center dataset comprising 10,970 ultrasound frames acquired from 58 pregnant women during routine neurosonographic examinations across three clinical sites using heterogeneous imaging devices. The proposed framework achieved strong performance in the federated setting. In particular, the combination of DINOv2 and LoRA under the FedAvg strategy achieved an average mAP@50 of 0.857 and an F1-score of 0.803, outperforming both full fine-tuning and encoder-freezing baselines. Notably, the proposed approach reduced the number of trainable parameters to 2.9M compared with 24.4M in full fine-tuning, corresponding to an approximately 8.5$\times$ reduction in communication cost. These findings represent a promising step toward scalable, privacy-preserving, and clinically deployable AI systems for fetal neurosonography.

</details>


### [80] [Compressing What Matters: Neuron Importance Meets Data-Aware Low Rank Approximation for Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18284)
*Athanasios Ntovas,Alexandros Doumanoglou,Petros Drakoulis,Dimitris Zarpalas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合参数重要性和逐层功能等价性的SVD压缩方法，并设计了一种高效动态压缩率分配算法，在高压缩比下表现优于或持平于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数规模庞大，内存需求高，限制了其在资源受限环境下的应用。现有SVD压缩方法要么从参数重要性角度出发，要么从逐层功能等价性角度出发，未将两者结合；且压缩率分配多采用均匀分配或计算成本高昂的启发式搜索，缺乏高效方案。

Method: (1) 将参数重要性与逐层功能等价性两种视角融合到单一目标函数中进行低秩近似压缩； (2) 提出一种增强且计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法，替代均匀分配或昂贵启发式搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在高压缩比下与现有最优方法性能相当或显著更优。

Conclusion: 结合两种视角并采用高效动态压缩率分配的策略能够有效提升SVD压缩质量，尤其在高度压缩场景下优势明显。

Abstract: To excel at their domain large language models are comprised of billions of parameters. Yet this comes at the cost of huge memory requirements restricting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. To address the problem of neural network (NN) compression Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) has played a key role as a fundamental component for matrix compression through decomposition. To minimize compression error and to maximize the efficacy of the compressed model on the downstream tasks previous works focused on low-rank approximation of the NN's weight matrices either from the perspective of parameter importance or per-layer functional equivalence. While previous works studied the aforementioned perspectives in isolation in this work we are investigating the effectiveness of an approach that combines ideas from these two perspectives in a single objective. In parallel to this an important aspect that affects the compression quality is the distribution of the compression rate across layers and NN parameters. Earlier works mostly considered distributing the compression rate uniformly across layers and network weights or relied on computationally expensive heuristic search. Contrary to them in this work we propose an enhanced and computationally efficient algorithm for dynamic compression rate allocation. Experimental results support the efficacy of the proposed approach which performs on par or substantially better than the previous state-of-the-art especially under high compression ratios.

</details>


### [81] [PAC--Bayes Bounds on Quotient Parameter Spaces: Geometry-induced Implicit-Bias Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18422)
*Nicola Aladrah,Fabio Anselmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在过参数化模型的PAC-Bayes分析中，应在商预测器空间上操作，并通过构造规范参数化和考虑几何体积来构建数据无关的隐式偏差先验，从而得到更紧的证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化模型存在连续参数对称性，不同参数可定义相同预测器。标准PAC-Bayes分析在参数空间进行时，KL项会包含仅由同一预测器不同参数化差异带来的非负贡献，导致证书过松。需要在商预测器空间进行分析以消除该冗余。

Method: 1) 将先验和后验推到商预测器空间，保留经验风险和总体Gibbs风险，移除参数化冗余带来的KL贡献。2) 构造规范参数化作为每个预测器的代表参数化，并计算其等价参数化集合的几何体积，将中性参考先验转化为反映模型隐式偏差的数据无关先验。3) 该先验近似理论上最优但不可行的后验匹配先验（依赖训练数据最小化KL项）。

Result: 在Fourier-Hadamard参数化回归中，隐式偏差先验使商空间KL均值降低40.69%，PAC-Bayes证书均值降低21.40%。在Query-Key注意力机制中观察到较小且依赖先验尺度的改进，验证了效应的条件性预测。

Conclusion: 商预测器空间上的PAC-Bayes分析消除了参数化冗余，而基于几何体积构建的隐式偏差先验能在无显式正则化的情况下提供更紧的泛化证书，且效果与模型结构相关。

Abstract: Overparameterized models often have continuous parameter symmetries, so different parameters define the same predictor. We show that PAC--Bayesian analysis should be performed on the quotient predictor space: pushing a prior and posterior to the quotient preserves the empirical and population Gibbs risks while removing the nonnegative KL contribution caused solely by how the two distributions differ among parameterizations of the same predictor.
  Quotienting alone does not determine which prior to use. We construct a canonical choice of one parameterization for each predictor and account for the geometric volume of its equivalent parameterizations. This transforms a neutral reference prior into a data-independent prior that reflects the model's implicit bias. It approximates the ideal but inadmissible posterior-matched prior, which would minimize the KL term by depending on the training data. The resulting certificate is tighter exactly when this geometry-induced prior has smaller KL divergence from the learned quotient posterior than the neutral prior.
  We test this prediction in Fourier regression with a Hadamard parameterization and in Query-Key attention, using ordinary SGD without an explicit regularizer. The implicit-bias prior reduces the mean quotient-space KL by \(40.69\%\) and the mean PAC--Bayes certificate by \(21.40\%\) in the Fourier-Hadamard experiment. The smaller, prior-scale-dependent improvement in Query-Key attention confirms the predicted conditional nature of the effect.

</details>


### [82] [Edge-Efficient Transformer for End-to-End RF Spectrum Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18285)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E-SpecFormer（Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer），用于端到端自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别。其核心创新是LiTAN（Linear Tanh Attention Network）——一种无需Softmax和LayerNorm的注意力机制，在降低计算复杂度的同时提升射频任务的准确率。E-SpecFormer有四种可扩展变体（Nano, Small, Medium, Large）以适应不同硬件约束。在RadioML2018数据集上，Nano变体在SNR>0 dB时达到86.5%的平均准确率；在基于硬件木马的隐蔽信道数据集上达到94.2%的准确率，参数量少于10k，在FPGA/CPU协同执行下每帧仅需92 μs，以极低成本超越了现有边缘模型。E-SpecFormer为物联网设备上的实时频谱智能提供了高效边缘解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在射频（RF）任务（如调制识别和隐蔽信道检测）中虽有成效，但通常计算复杂度过高、参数量大，难以部署在资源受限的物联网边缘设备上。需要一种轻量级、高效率的模型，在保持高准确率的同时满足实时性要求。

Method: 提出E-SpecFormer架构，核心是LiTAN（Linear Tanh Attention Network）注意力机制，该机制摒弃了Softmax和LayerNorm，采用线性Tanh操作，降低计算复杂度并提升RF任务准确率。模型提供四种可扩展变体（Nano, Small, Medium, Large）以适应不同硬件约束。采用FPGA/CPU协同执行实现高效推理。

Result: 在RadioML2018调制识别数据集上，Nano变体在SNR>0 dB时达到86.5%平均准确率；在基于硬件木马的隐蔽信道数据集上达到94.2%准确率。参数量少于10k，FPGA/CPU协同执行下每帧处理速度达92 μs，在准确率和效率上均超越了现有最先进的边缘模型。

Conclusion: E-SpecFormer证明了在极低参数量和极快推理速度下，边缘设备上的实时频谱智能是可行的，为物联网设备上的自动调制识别和隐蔽信道检测提供了高效、可部署的边缘解决方案。

Abstract: We present E-SpecFormer (Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer) for end-to-end automatic modulation and covert channel (CC) recognition. We introduce LiTAN (Linear Tanh Attention Network), a Softmax- and LayerNorm-free attention mechanism that reduces complexity while increasing accuracy in RF tasks. E-SpecFormer is parameterized in four scalable variants (Nano, Small, Medium, Large) to accommodate diverse hardware constraints. Using the RadioML2018 dataset for modulation recognition, the Nano variant achieves 86.5% average accuracy for Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs)>0 dB, and on the hardware Trojan (HT)-based CC dataset it reaches 94.2% accuracy, both with fewer than 10k parameters and up to speed of 92 μs per frame on FPGA/CPU co-execution, surpassing state-of-the-art edge models at a fraction of their cost. These results establish E-SpecFormer as an edge-efficient solution for real-time spectrum intelligence on Internet of Things (IoT) devices. GitHub link to the repository: https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer.

</details>


### [83] [Elicitation without Backpropagation: Steering Model Behavior by Optimizing the Latent Posterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18804)
*Garrett Baker,Vinayak Pathak,Daniel Murfet,Susan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出后验前缀调优（PPT）方法，利用贝叶斯滤波变换器（BFT）的潜在后验模型，通过从先验中采样并仅使用重要性采样估计梯度，无需前向传播或反向传播即可优化硬提示分布，从而高效地引导变换器生成高期望效用的续文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在变换器的潜在后验模型中，下一个词元的分布来源于以上下文为条件的潜在预测模型后验。作者希望利用这一精确模型（特别是在贝叶斯滤波变换器中），开发一种无需大量计算开销即可引导变换器产生高期望效用行为的方法。

Method: PPT方法通过优化硬提示分布参数来工作。它首先通过预测性蒙特卡洛（PMC）从BFT中抽取一次先验样本，然后通过重要性采样估计梯度，无需任何变换器前向传播或反向传播。由于先验样本与效用无关，一组样本即可用于任意多个效用的引导，边际成本可忽略不计。

Result: 在Beta-伯努利和强化瓮BFT上，针对三种效用族（反向交叉熵、频率匹配、Dyck有效性）的验证实验表明，PPT方法能够有效引导变换器生成高期望效用的续文。

Conclusion: PPT是一种高效、可扩展的提示调优方法，利用BFT的潜在后验结构实现了与效用无关的先验采样和零额外计算成本的多次效用引导，为变换器行为引导提供了新思路。

Abstract: In the \emph{latent posterior model} of transformer behavior, the next-token distribution arises from a posterior over latent predictive models conditioned on the context, mixed to generate continuations. We exploit this model in settings where it is exact, namely Bayes-filtered transformers (BFTs) meta-learned on sequences from a hierarchical prior, to introduce \textbf{Posterior Prefix Tuning (PPT)}, a new method for \emph{eliciting} behavior from a transformer: given a utility function on continuations, find a prompt under which the transformer generates continuations of high expected utility. For a BFT, the elicitation objective factors through the latent posterior, and the gradient of this objective can be estimated from samples of the prior alone. PPT optimizes the parameters of a distribution over hard prompts: it draws prior samples once from the BFT via predictive Monte Carlo (PMC), then estimates the gradient by importance sampling against them. The optimization performs no transformer forward passes and no backpropagation through the transformer, and the prior samples are utility-independent, so a single set of samples drives elicitation against any number of utilities at negligible marginal cost. We validate PPT on Beta--Bernoulli and reinforced urn BFTs across three utility families (reverse cross-entropy, frequency matching, Dyck validity).

</details>


### [84] [Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Runtime-Tunable Transit Signal Priority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18286)
*Philip-Roman Adam,Stefanie Schmidtner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种偏好条件化公交信号优先（TSP）控制器，通过运行时调整偏好参数w来平衡公交优先与整体交通延误，无需重新训练，实验表明其性能优于固定权重策略和规则基线，且约束可行性良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习TSP方法通常优化固定奖励或标量化函数，当机构优先级因时段或干扰条件变化时缺乏操作灵活性，无法在运行时灵活调整公交优先与交通延误的权衡。

Method: 提出偏好条件化TSP控制器π(a|s,w)，在最小/最大绿灯时间和过渡可行性约束下选择下一信号相位，通过偏好参数w运行时调节公交优先程度。基于IntersectionZoo实现，引入约束信号控制/TSP封装，扩展场景生成（公交密度增强、基于时刻表的公交插入）以解决训练中稀疏公交事件问题。

Result: 单个学习到的条件化策略在运行时偏好参数下覆盖平滑的经验权衡前沿，优于固定时间和规则基线，保持约束可行性。尾部延迟诊断显示中等偏好设置下非公交外部性有限，但高公交优先权重下会显著增加。

Conclusion: 偏好条件化TSP策略能够在不重新训练的情况下灵活适应不同优先级场景，为实际TSP部署提供了更灵活的权衡机制。

Abstract: Transit signal priority (TSP) requires balancing competing objectives: reducing bus delay while limiting adverse impacts on non-bus traffic and avoiding extreme waits for a subset of vehicles. Existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches to TSP typically encode transit-aware features (e.g., occupancy and schedule deviation) but optimize a fixed reward or fixed scalarization, which limits operational flexibility when agency priorities change across time-of-day or disruption conditions. We present a preference-conditioned TSP controller, $π(a \mid s,w)$, that selects the next signal phase under minimum/maximum green and transition-feasibility constraints and can be tuned at runtime via a preference parameter $w$ to trade off bus-priority emphasis against overall traffic delay without retraining. We implement this on top of IntersectionZoo by introducing a constrained signal-control/TSP wrapper, and we extend scenario generation with bus-prevalence augmentation and timetable-based bus insertion to address sparse transit-priority events during training. Experiments against fixed-time control, a rule-based TSP overlay, and fixed-weight PPO specialists show that a single learned conditioned policy spans a smooth empirical trade-off frontier across runtime preferences, outperforms fixed-time and rule-based baselines, and maintains constraint feasibility, while tail-delay diagnostics reveal that non-bus externalities remain limited for moderate preference settings but can increase substantially under high bus-priority weights. The source code of this work is available at https://github.com/urbanAIthi/morl-tsp.

</details>


### [85] [Deep learning-based prediction of time-resolved adhesive forces in viscoelastic Hertzian contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19060)
*Ali Maghami,Merten Stender,Michele Ciavarella,Antonio Papangelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于序列到序列深度学习模型的粘弹性软接触力响应快速预测方法，通过固定测量步长表示和LSTM架构，实现了在宽参数范围内（Tabor参数0.2-3.2，速率跨越四个数量级）对完整力轨迹的毫秒级预测，中位拉脱力误差约2.2%，滞后误差约1.1%，推理时间0.16秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性软接触的完整时变力响应预测依赖全数值模拟，计算成本高且强烈依赖参数，无法满足软体机器人、抓取操作等实时应用或设计优化循环的需求。

Method: 1) 构建scalar-conditioned、stateful、sequence-to-sequence深度学习模型；2) 引入固定测量步长（FMS）表示法，将变长轨迹转换为固定长度序列；3) 对比LSTM、时序卷积网络（TCN）和时间分布式稠密层三种架构，结合三种不同的Tabor参数调节机制；4) 数据集覆盖加载/卸载速率四个数量级、不同驻留时间，Tabor参数0.2-3.2。

Result: 最佳模型为LSTM+拼接调节架构，在保留测试集上均方误差5.0×10⁻⁴，中位拉脱力误差≈2.2%，中位滞后误差≈1.1%，完整力轨迹推理中位时间0.16秒。

Conclusion: 该模型可在未见参数组合及解析极限情况下进行快速预测，作为全数值模拟的高效替代，适用于控制导向的应用场景。

Abstract: Fast prediction of the response of adhesive soft viscoelastic contacts represents a current challenge in soft robotics and for gripping and manipulation tasks. Determining the complete time-resolved force trajectory requires full numerical simulations, whose computational cost is strongly parameter-dependent, making them impractical for real-time application or design-optimization loops. In this work, we overcome this limitation by training a scalar-conditioned, stateful, sequence-to-sequence deep learning model to predict the full force evolution from a prescribed displacement history for both short- and long-range adhesion regimes. The data set spans four orders of magnitude in loading and unloading rates and includes varied dwell times, with the Tabor parameter ranging from $0.2$ to $3.2$. To enable learning across these heterogeneous time scales, we introduce a fixed-measurement-step (FMS) representation that converts variable-length trajectories into fixed-length sequences while preserving their physical-time information. Different architectures were trained, including long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, temporal convolutional neural (TCN) networks, and time-distributed dense layers with three different Tabor-conditioning mechanisms. The models were compared using global waveform and error metrics. We found that the best-performing model has an LSTM architecture with concatenated conditioning, which achieves a held-out mean-squared error of $5.0\times10^{-4}$, a median pull-off-force error of $\approx2.2\%$, and a median hysteresis error of $\approx1.1\%$. For the held-out protocols, the model predicts a complete force trajectory with a median inference time of $0.16$ s. The model is tested across unseen parameter combinations and against analytical limiting cases, providing a rapid surrogate for repeated numerical evaluations with potential use in control-oriented applications.

</details>


### [86] [BearingNAS: Obtaining In-Sensor Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems for Bearings Using a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18287)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli,Rodolfo Zunino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BearingNAS是一个面向极端微预算（4-8 KiB RAM，16-32 KiB Flash）的硬件感知神经架构搜索框架，专为在传感器芯片内进行轴承故障诊断设计。该框架采用无导数搜索策略和衰减核增长搜索空间，在笔记本电脑CPU上不到一小时即可完成搜索，在LSM6DSO16IS智能传感器处理单元上达到99.50%的诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经架构搜索方法依赖昂贵的离散GPU，且生成的模型通常无法在极端资源受限的传感器芯片上运行。本文旨在将机器学习推理能力直接迁移到传感器芯片内部，实现低成本、可量产的在传感器处理方案。

Method: 将搜索建模为约束优化问题；提出轻量级无导数搜索策略替代GPU依赖；设计具有衰减核增长机制的单数据流搜索空间以防止参数爆炸；针对STMicroelectronics的三个目标平台（两个通用微控制器和LSM6DSO16IS智能传感器处理单元）进行优化。

Result: 在笔记本电脑CPU上搜索收敛时间不超过1小时；在CWRU轴承基准测试上，最佳在传感器架构在ISPU上达到99.50%的诊断准确率。

Conclusion: 证明了在传感器封装内实现机器学习工作负载的可行性，为低成本的量产级轴承故障诊断提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces BearingNAS, a Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search (HW-NAS) framework designed to shift the intelligence directly onto the sensor die via in-sensor processing. BearingNAS frames the search as a constrained optimization problem targeting extreme micro-budgets (4 to 8 kiB of RAM and 16 to 32 kiB of Flash). To eliminate the reliance on expensive discrete GPUs, we propose a lightweight, derivative-free search strategy paired with a single data-flow search space that leverages a decaying kernel growth formulation to prevent parameter explosion. We evaluate our framework on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) bearing benchmark, optimizing architectures for three STMicroelectronics targets: two commodity microcontrollers and the LSM6DSO16IS Intelligent Sensor Processing Unit (ISPU). Running entirely on a laptop CPU, the search converges in less than an hour. The resulting best in-sensor architecture achieves a highly competitive diagnostic accuracy of 99.50\% on the ISPU. These results demonstrate the viability of shifting the machine learning workload inside the sensor package, enabling low-cost, production-scale bearing fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [87] [Provable diffusion-based posterior sampling for linear inverse problems via DDIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19333)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuxin Chen,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PDDIM算法，通过对标准DDIM更新进行轻量级、逐坐标的修改来求解线性逆问题，沿测量算子的每个奇异方向分别进行后验采样，在信噪比低于扩散信噪比时遵循学习到的扩散先验，否则切换到基于测量值的预测器。理论上证明该方法收敛于贝叶斯后验，实验表明在多个图像恢复任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的后验采样器要么缺乏严格的理论保证，要么计算开销过大。作者希望设计一种兼具理论严谨性和计算效率的算法。

Method: PDDIM算法：对DDIM更新进行逐坐标修改，沿测量算子的每个奇异方向分别处理，根据观测信噪比与扩散信噪比的比较来决定使用扩散先验还是基于测量值的预测器。

Result: 理论证明该方法收敛于给定测量条件下的贝叶斯后验；实验在多项图像恢复任务中，该方法在大多数评估指标上优于现有的扩散后验采样器。

Conclusion: 将带噪线性逆问题的后验采样转化为简单的逐坐标DDIM更新，提供了一种高效、易实现且具有后验一致性的算法。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have achieved remarkable empirical success in solving inverse problems. However, many existing posterior samplers either lack rigorous theoretical guarantees or incur substantial computational overhead. We propose a simple and efficient algorithm, called \pddim, for solving linear inverse problems with diffusion priors via a DDIM-type sampler. Our method requires only lightweight, coordinate-wise modifications to the standard DDIM update, while explicitly incorporating the measurement model. The key idea is to perform posterior sampling separately along each singular direction of the measurement operator: for each direction, the sampler follows the learned diffusion prior when the observation signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is below the corresponding diffusion SNR, and switches to a calibrated measurement-based predictor otherwise. We prove that the proposed sampler converges to the Bayesian posterior conditioned on the measurements. Empirical results show that the proposed sampler performs favorably against existing diffusion-based posterior samplers across a range of image restoration tasks, achieving the best performance on the majority of evaluation metrics considered. Overall, our results convert posterior sampling for noisy linear inverse problems to simple coordinate-wise DDIM updates, yielding an efficient, easy-to-implement algorithm with provable posterior consistency.

</details>


### [88] [Multi-Timescale Latent-Action DRL for Joint Optimization in Edge-Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18288)
*Vo Phi Son,Van-Dinh Nguyen,Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Trinh Van Chien,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分层边云协同计算系统中的负载不均衡问题，提出了一种基于两时间尺度多层级深度强化学习与潜在动作空间的框架（2T-MDRL-LA），联合优化服务部署、计算委派和功率控制，以最小化端到端平均延迟。实验表明该方法在延迟降低（最高20.8%）、资源利用率提升（13%）和收敛速度（比PPO快约50%）方面均具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层边云协同计算系统中，动态任务到达和异构资源导致跨边云层的负载不均衡，进而加剧排队延迟并降低资源利用率，亟需一种联合优化方案来最小化端到端平均延迟。

Method: 将联合服务部署、计算委派和功率控制（JSCP）问题建模为混合整数非凸NP-hard优化问题，利用决策动力学差异将问题分解为长期系统配置和短期资源分配两个子问题，并提出两时间尺度多层级深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），引入基于变分自编码器的潜在动作空间以压缩高维组合动作空间。

Result: 所提框架能有效适应动态网络条件，性能接近分支定界最优解；相比无计算委派方案，平均端到端延迟降低20.8%，资源利用率提升13%；收敛速度比传统PPO算法快约50%。

Conclusion: 2T-MDRL-LA框架能有效解决HECC系统中多变量强耦合的联合优化难题，在延迟、资源利用率和收敛效率方面均优于现有方案，为边云协同系统的在线自适应调度提供了一种高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Load imbalance across edge and cloud layers degrades latency performance in hierarchical edge-cloud computing (HECC) systems under dynamic task arrivals and heterogeneous resources, leading to severe queuing delays and inefficient resource utilization. To address this challenge, we study a joint service placement, computational delegation, and power control (JSCP) problem to minimize the average end-to-end (e2e) latency. The resulting JSCP problem is a mixed-integer nonconvex and NP-hard optimization problem due to the strong coupling between discrete and continuous variables. To enable tractable optimization and stable system adaptation, we exploit the inherent difference in decision dynamics and decompose the problem into long-term system configuration and short-term resource allocation subproblems. Based on this formulation, we propose a two-timescale multi-layer deep reinforcement learning framework with a latent action space (2T-MDRL-LA) to jointly optimize service placement, user association, computational delegation, task offloading, and user transmit power. A latent action representation based on a variational autoencoder is introduced to efficiently compress the high-dimensional combinatorial action space. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively adapts to dynamic network conditions and achieves near-optimal performance compared to branch-and-bound solutions. It achieves up to a 20.8% reduction in average e2e latency and a 13% improvement in resource utilization over the scheme without the computational delegation, while converging approximately 50% faster than conventional proximal policy optimization.

</details>


### [89] [Towards Principled Continual Anomaly Detection: A Systematic Framework and Benchmark Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18289)
*Kamil Faber,Mateusz Smendowski,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统化框架，用于从现有表格异常检测数据集中设计可复现的持续异常检测基准场景，包括任务发现、过滤和排序，并在三个网络安全数据集上构建了五个基准场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续异常检测（CAD）基准测试高度依赖任务的定义、过滤、排序和验证方式，而在表格领域中任务边界极少被明确给出，任意分割可能产生不可学习、冗余或过度可迁移的任务，掩盖真正的持续学习行为，因此需要系统化的基准场景设计方法。

Method: 提出一个系统化框架，从现有表格异常检测数据集中发现候选任务、过滤不合适的任务，并推导出能够暴露多样化学习动态的原则性排序，最终生成可复现的基准场景。

Result: 从三个大规模网络安全异常检测数据集中提取出五个基准测试场景，涵盖单数据集和多数据集的持续异常检测设置。

Conclusion: 该框架能够系统化地生成有意义的CAD基准场景，解决了表格领域中任务划分随意性的问题，有助于更真实地评估持续学习行为。

Abstract: Continual anomaly detection (CAD) studies how models can adapt to evolving data distributions while retaining performance on previously observed regimes. CAD benchmarks, however, depend critically on how tasks are defined, filtered, ordered, and validated. In tabular domains, task boundaries are rarely given, and arbitrary splits can create unlearnable, redundant, or overly transferable tasks that obscure genuine continual-learning behavior. To this end, we introduce a systematic framework for reproducible benchmark scenario design from existing tabular anomaly-detection datasets. The framework discovers candidate tasks, filters unsuitable tasks, and derives principled orderings that expose diverse dynamics. The framework allows us to deliver five benchmark-ready scenarios from three large-scale cybersecurity anomaly detection datasets, yielding both single-dataset and multi-dataset CAD settings.

</details>


### [90] [SechKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Hyperbolic Secant Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18290)
*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SechKAN，一种基于双曲正割函数的KAN架构，在函数拟合、PDE问题和图像分类任务上优于MLP和其他KAN变体，参数规模与MLP相当，但运行时略长于MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN在网络设计中展现出新的范式，但现有KAN变体存在参数过多或性能不足的问题，需要设计一种兼具高效参数利用和优越性能的KAN架构。

Method: 采用双曲正割(sech)函数作为基函数，利用其光滑钟形、局部响应和稳定梯度的特性；通过一维线性变换减少参数量，使模型大小与MLP相当。

Result: 在函数拟合、PDE问题以及MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100四个图像分类基准数据集上，SechKAN在参数数量相近的条件下性能优于MLP和其他KAN变体；运行时间优于其他KAN变体但略慢于MLP。

Conclusion: SechKAN是一种有效的KAN架构，在保持参数效率的同时实现了优越的性能，是MLP和其他KAN变体的有力替代方案。

Abstract: In recent years, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have attracted increasing attention due to their effectiveness in machine learning and scientific computing tasks, offering a new paradigm for neural network design. In this paper, we present SechKAN, a KAN architecture based on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions. The hyperbolic secant basis is used for its smooth bell-shaped form, localized responses, and stable gradients. We employ 1D linear transformations to reduce the number of parameters, allowing SechKAN to remain comparable to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in model size. Experimental results indicate the effectiveness of SechKAN in function fitting, PDE problems, and image classification tasks on benchmark datasets, including MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100. SechKAN achieves superior performance compared to MLPs and other KAN variants while maintaining a similar number of parameters. However, its running time, while better than that of other KAN variants, is slightly longer than that of MLPs.

</details>


### [91] [Dual-domain fused LSTM modeling for efficient time-dependent reliability analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18291)
*Yixin Zhang,Mingyang Li,Zichao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双域融合长短期记忆（DDF-LSTM）模型，通过将时不变变量嵌入LSTM初始隐藏状态并引入全连接层融合输出，结合改进损失函数，实现了高效、高精度的时变可靠性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代理模型方法难以同时处理时不变随机变量及其与时变随机过程之间的复杂交互作用，导致时变可靠性分析的精度和效率受限。

Method: 构建DDF-LSTM网络架构：将时不变变量嵌入LSTM初始隐藏状态；引入全连接层映射LSTM输出与时不变变量到最终输出空间；设计改进的损失函数增强模型对最小响应的敏感性。

Result: 通过四个案例验证，所提方法在计算效率和预测精度上均有显著提升，训练后的DDF-LSTM模型能以极低计算成本进行蒙特卡洛仿真，估计时变失效概率。

Conclusion: DDF-LSTM有效捕获了随机变量、随机过程与极限状态函数时间行为之间的依赖关系，为时变可靠性分析提供了一种高效精准的解决方案。

Abstract: Time-dependent reliability analysis is crucial for ensuring the long-term safety and performance of engineering systems under uncertainties. However, traditional surrogate model methods often struggle to incorporate time-independent random variables and capture their complex interactions with time-dependent stochastic processes. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a dual-domain fused long short-term memory (DDF-LSTM) model for efficient and accurate time-dependent reliability analysis. A novel network architecture is developed to jointly process information from both time-dependent and time-independent domains. Specifically, the time-independent variables are embedded into the initial hidden states, and a fully connected layer is introduced to map both LSTM outputs and time-independent variables into the final output space. Furthermore, an improved loss function is designed to emphasize the model's sensitivity to minimum responses, thereby improving the precision of failure probability estimation. The proposed method effectively captures the dependencies among random variables, stochastic processes, and the temporal behavior of limit state functions. Once trained, the DDF-LSTM model enables efficient Monte Carlo simulation to estimate time-dependent failure probabilities with minimal computational cost. Four case studies validate the proposed method's enhanced computational efficiency and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [92] [Real-time optimal control with shallow recurrent decoder networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19302)
*Matteo Tomasetto,Francesco Braghin,J. Nathan Kutz,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SHRED-ROM的实时闭环控制策略，利用少量传感器数据驱动高维参数化动力学系统，通过模仿专家示范实现跨场景自适应控制，并在三个高维案例（密度控制、流体控制）中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优控制问题需要大量系统仿真，计算成本高，难以实时应对高维时空动力学系统的多场景自适应控制需求。

Method: 采用SHRED-ROM（浅层循环解码器网络降阶模型）构建实时闭环控制器，仅依赖有限状态传感器读数；训练时使用少量专家示范的最优示例进行模仿学习；同时构建传感器预测器在潜空间层面闭环，以应对传感器故障或延迟。

Result: 在三个高维挑战性案例（参数化密度控制、流体流动控制）上验证了所提策略的有效性，实现了跨场景的有效分布式控制，缓解了维度灾难。

Conclusion: SHRED-ROM能够以极低的计算代价实现高维参数化动力学系统的实时闭环最优控制，具有较好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Controlling dynamical systems in real-time across multiple scenarios is critical to enabling adaptive control strategies, ensuring stability and efficiency. However, to tailor control actions in response to varying scenarios, traditional optimal control problems typically require several system simulations, which are often computationally demanding due to the high-dimensionality of the underlying spatio-temporal dynamics. In this work, we exploit SHallow REcurrent Decoder networks-based Reduced Order Modeling (SHRED-ROM) to synthesize a real-time closed-loop controller for high-dimensional and parametric dynamics, relying solely on limited state sensor readings. After training the model on a few optimal examples given by an expert demonstrator, SHRED-ROM mimics the expert behavior with effective distributed control actions in new scenarios, alleviating the curse of dimensionality. Moreover, a sensor forecaster is synthesized and used to close the loop at the latent level, thus efficiently mitigating possible sensor failures or delays. The performance of the proposed optimal control strategy is finally assessed on three challenging high-dimensional cases dealing with either parametric density control or fluid flow control.

</details>


### [93] [Reliability Scales Inversely: Bigger Models Compound Mistakes Faster via a Hidden Auto-Regressive Risk Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18292)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 随着语言模型规模扩大，答案起初更准确但退化更快：扩展提升能力却侵蚀可靠性。知识差距假说（更多数据、检索或规模）忽略了自回归风险残差，而规模放大了它：模型承诺一个低概率token，将其视为既定条件，然后雪崩式恶化。我们通过每个位置的差异δ=log p_M - log p_O（相对于更强的同族oracle）来追踪这一点，其二阶矩恰好分解为偏差² KL(p_M∥p_O)²和风险Var[δ]。我们提出四个发现：(i) 在扩展下，知识差距下降约6倍，而知识退化增长11-39倍；(ii) 在虚构处，模型的感知不确定性H(p_M)迅速放松，而oracle参考的风险持续长达17倍，留下一个自信但危险的风险区间，桥接连续虚构（140亿参数下+69%）；(iii) 该区间是因果性的——一种在策略上、固定KL的方差收缩使网络验证的幻觉减少35-74%（跨三个模型家族）；(iv) 它在结构上规避自我监控，仅依赖p_M的检测器（如语义熵）在承载近4倍虚构的风险分支上触发率低约30%（p<10^{-16}）。更大的模型更快地雪崩式犯错，通过一种主导性、自我延续、因果性且对模型自身不可见的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为通过扩大数据、增加检索或增大模型规模可以解决大语言模型的幻觉问题（知识差距假说），但作者观察到规模扩大反而导致模型错误更快地雪崩式恶化。本文旨在揭示被传统知识差距视角忽视的一个关键机制：自回归过程中的风险残差——模型在生成过程中一旦承诺一个低概率token，会将其作为条件继续生成，导致错误逐级放大。

Method: 作者提出per-position disagreement δ = log p_M - log p_O的度量框架，其中p_M是目标模型概率，p_O是同族更强大的oracle模型概率。δ的二阶矩分解为偏差² KL(p_M∥p_O)²和风险Var[δ]。通过跟踪δ的偏置和方差成分，区分"知识差距"（模型因缺乏知识而产生的偏差）和"知识退化"（因自回归条件错误导致的风险方差）。在不同规模模型上进行实验，并设计基于固定KL的方差收缩干预来验证因果关系。

Result: (i) 模型扩展时，知识差距下降约6倍，但知识退化增长11-39倍；(ii) 虚构时模型自身不确定性H(p_M)迅速下降，但oracle参考风险持续更久（长达17倍），形成自信但危险的风险区间，该区间桥接连续虚构（140亿参数下增加69%）；(iii) 通过固定KL的方差收缩干预，可在三个模型家族上将网络验证的幻觉减少35-74%；(iv) 仅依赖p_M的检测器（如语义熵）在此风险分支上触发率低约30%，而该分支承载近4倍更多的虚构。

Conclusion: 更大的模型以更快的速度雪崩式犯错，这种失败模式具有四个特征：主导性（规模扩大后占主导）、自我延续（错误自生成条件下逐级放大）、因果性（干预该机制可直接减少幻觉）、对模型自身不可见（模型自身的置信度指标无法检测到这一风险）。传统依赖于模型自身概率的幻觉检测方法系统性失效，需要引入外部oracle参考来识别这类风险。

Abstract: As language models scale, answers start truer but degrade faster: scaling buys capability but erodes reliability. The knowledge-gap account - more data, retrieval, or scale - misses an auto-regressive risk residual that scale sharpens: the model commits to a low-probability token, conditions on it as established, and snowballs. We track this through per-position disagreement $δ= \log p_M - \log p_O$ against a stronger same-family oracle, whose second moment splits exactly into bias$^2$ $\mathrm{KL}(p_M \,\|\, p_O)^2$ and risk $\mathrm{Var}[δ]$. We present four findings: (i) under scaling, the knowledge gap falls $\approx$$6\times$ while knowledge degradation grows $11$-$39\times$; (ii) at a fabrication, felt uncertainty $H(p_M)$ relaxes quickly while oracle-referenced risk persists up to $17\times$ longer, leaving a confident-but-precarious risk regime that bridges consecutive fabrications ($+69\%$ at $14$B); (iii) this regime is causal - an on-policy, fixed-$\mathrm{KL}$ variance contraction cuts web-verified hallucination by $35$-$74\%$ across three model families; and, (iv) it structurally evades self-monitoring, with $p_M$-only detectors (e.g. semantic entropy) firing $\approx$$30\%$ less ($p<10^{-16}$) on the risky branch holding nearly $4\times$ more fabrications. Bigger models snowball mistakes faster, through a failure mode that is dominant, self-perpetuating, causal and invisible to the model itself.

</details>


### [94] [One Student, Many Teachers: Multi-Task On-Policy Distillation via Soft-Prompt Privileged Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18293)
*Yingzi Ma,Zichen Zhu,Ming Jiang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可学习软提示的教师模型（Prompt-Tuning Teacher, PT Teacher），用于同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）。该教师仅通过软提示与骨干网络交互，保持学生模型的表征几何不变，支持多任务并行蒸馏，在多个基准上超越全参数微调，同时避免灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏方法中，教师要么在输入中注入特权上下文导致事后合理化，要么微调权重导致漂移和跨任务遗忘。需要一种既能提供任务特定指导、又不破坏学生模型表征的教师设计。

Method: 教师仅通过在冻结的骨干网络上添加可学习的软提示（soft prompt）来定义，该提示在(x, y_gold)上训练。多任务场景中，每个样本路由到对应的软提示教师，学生模型并行吸收K个教师的知识，推理时丢弃所有提示。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B-Base和Phi-4-mini-instruct上，单任务变体匹配或超越全参数微调，但训练参数量少数个数量级；多任务变体在四个任务上取得最佳平均分（Qwen3-1.7B-Base为56.2），同时保持通用能力，而序列微调会导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 基于软提示的教师模型能够在不改变学生表征几何的前提下实现有效的自蒸馏，且天然支持多任务并行学习，避免了序列微调的遗忘问题，是一种高效且可扩展的蒸馏范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) teaches large language models new skills through a teacher that shares the student's backbone and supervises its own rollouts. Existing teachers either inject privileged context at the input -- inducing post-hoc rationalization -- or fine-tune weights, accumulating drift and forgetting across tasks. We propose \method, whose teacher differs from the student only by a learnable soft prompt: trained on $(x, y_\text{gold})$ pairs with the backbone frozen, the prompt yields a task-specific teacher that preserves the student's exact representational geometry. \method\ extends naturally to multi-task settings by routing each example in a merged corpus to its corresponding soft-prompt teacher, allowing a single student to absorb knowledge from $K$ teachers in parallel; at inference, all prompts are discarded. On Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Phi-4-mini-instruct across four tasks (Science, Tool Use, Biology, Math), the single-task variant (OPD with a PT teacher) matches or exceeds full fine-tuning while training orders of magnitude fewer parameters, and the multi-task variant achieves the best overall average ($56.2$ on Qwen3-1.7B-Base) while preserving general-capability benchmarks -- in contrast to sequential SFT, which degrades both.

</details>


### [95] [Uncertainty Quantification for AI-Driven Crash Simulation Surrogates: A Comparative Study of Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensemble on Open-Source Bumper Beam Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18294)
*Sudeep Chavare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了蒙特卡洛Dropout（使用具体Dropout自动学习丢弃率）和深度集成两种不确定性量化方法在基于PhysicsNeMo的汽车碰撞仿真代理模型中的应用，发现两者在预测精度与不确定性校准之间存在根本性权衡，挑战了深度集成作为不确定性量化黄金标准的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代理模型在安全关键工程中缺乏不确定性估计，导致工程师无法判断模型何时不可信任，这严重限制了其在安全关键工作流中的采用。

Method: 基于NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo构建开源代理流水线，对比蒙特卡洛Dropout（采用具体Dropout自动学习丢弃率）与深度集成两种不确定性量化方法，在汽车钢制保险杠碰撞问题上进行保持一致的留出测试集评估。

Result: 两种方法在点预测精度、不确定性带校准和计算成本上存在根本性权衡，深度集成并非默认的黄金标准，具体Dropout能以更低计算成本实现校准良好且无需超参数的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 在校准良好的、无超参数的不确定性估计可在完全开源工程工作流中以比集成方法低得多的计算成本实现，挑战了深度集成的黄金标准地位。

Abstract: Machine learning surrogate models are increasingly being explored in engineering product development to augment simulation-driven design, offering near-instantaneous predictions that complement computationally expensive high-fidelity analyses. However, a critical gap limits their adoption in safety-critical workflows: a point prediction without an accompanying uncertainty estimate cannot tell an engineer when the model should not be trusted. This work presents a systematic, head-to-head comparison of two widely used uncertainty quantification approaches -- Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensembles -- applied to an open-source surrogate pipeline built on NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo. A key contribution is the use of concrete dropout, a built-in PhysicsNeMo capability that eliminates the dropout rate as a manual hyperparameter by learning it end-to-end during training, directly addressing the most common criticism of Monte Carlo Dropout-based uncertainty quantification. Automotive crash simulation is used as the application domain, with a steel bumper beam impact problem serving as the benchmark. Both methods are evaluated on identical held-out simulations and compared on point accuracy, uncertainty band calibration, and computational cost. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between accuracy and calibration that challenges the common assumption that deep ensembles are the default gold standard for surrogate uncertainty quantification. The findings demonstrate that well-calibrated, hyperparameter-free uncertainty estimates are achievable within a fully open-source engineering workflow at a fraction of the computational cost of ensemble approaches.

</details>


### [96] [On the Limits of Support-Preserving Alignment and Bounded Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18295)
*Aryan Dutt,Rui Mao,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对齐方案与有界安全过滤器能否将大语言模型的有害行为概率降至零。通过理论分析和实证评估，发现尽管增加过滤计算量能降低有害输出率，但最终会稳定在一个非零的平台上，表明存在一个持久的有害行为下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究指出偏好对齐下有害行为可能持续存在，且外部过滤在最坏情况下计算困难。但尚不清楚在实际对齐流程中，能否完全消除有害行为而不仅仅是压制其最明显的形式。

Method: 形式化定义了支撑保持对齐算子与有界过滤算法（黑盒、白盒、统计查询访问），分析其逼近理想消除器的能力。结合计算与信息论论证，说明有界过滤可能无法消除基模型分布支持的所有有害输出。在OpenRouter上对开源和托管LLM进行实证评估。

Result: 理论分析表明有界过滤在给定约束下难以完全消除有害输出。实证结果发现，随着过滤计算量增加，有害输出率下降但持续稳定在非零水平，证实了经验性有害下限的存在。

Conclusion: 即使采用对齐方案与有界安全过滤器，大语言模型的有害行为也难以完全消除，只能压制到某个下限水平。这揭示了当前安全对齐策略的本质局限性。

Abstract: We study whether alignment schemes that reshape a base model's output distribution, combined with bounded safety filters, can drive the probability of harmful behavior to zero in modern large language models. Recent research suggests that harmful behaviors can persist under preference-based alignment and that external filtering can be computationally hard in the worst case, but it remains unclear whether practical alignment pipelines that largely preserve internal representations can eliminate harmful behavior entirely rather than merely suppressing its most visible forms. We formalize this setting using support-preserving alignment operators together with bounded filtering algorithms under black-box, white-box, and statistical-query access, and analyze their ability to approximate an ideal eliminator that removes all harmful mass. Building on this framework, we provide computational and information-theoretic arguments indicating that, under these constraints, bounded filtering may fail to eliminate all harmful outputs supported by the base model's distribution. To evaluate these limits empirically, we analyze a range of state-of-the-art open-weight and hosted LLMs accessed via OpenRouter under bounded black-box, white-box, and statistical-query filters on adversarial prompts drawn from curated cybersecurity scenarios and PKU-SafeRLHF. Across models, filter classes, and query budgets, the estimated harmful-output rate decreases with additional filtering compute but consistently plateaus above zero, suggesting a persistent empirical harm floor.

</details>


### [97] [A Better Start for Language Models: Domain-Conditional Position Offsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18302)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种域条件位置偏移（domain-conditional position offset），仅在第一序列位置添加一个学习向量，在不改变模型权重的情况下，显著降低自回归语言模型在序列起始处的困惑度（冷启动惩罚），最高可达27%的域内困惑度下降，且训练仅需几分钟、约一百篇文档，可热切换、零延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型在序列起始处因缺乏上下文，严重依赖预训练先验，导致准确率最低（冷启动惩罚）。作者发现该惩罚具有领域依赖性，因此希望设计一种轻量级方法在领域层面缓解该问题，而无需微调模型权重或引入额外序列状态。

Method: 提出域条件位置偏移（domain-conditional position offset）：一个可学习的向量，仅添加到序列第一位置的嵌入激活上，所有模型权重保持冻结。该偏移在约100篇文档上训练数分钟即可，可在不同领域间热切换，不增加序列状态，推理时无延迟开销。实验中在Mamba、GPT-NeoX、Llama等8个模型（410M~8B参数）上评估，并在70B模型上验证效果持续性。

Result: 域内困惑度最高降低27%；在70B模型上效果持续；单个位置即可捕获大部分收益。对比方法：直接logit偏置校正最多仅达7.9%且不影响后续token损失，表明偏移是通过传播模型状态而非仅重新校准输出先验。LoRA能达到更低困惑度但参数多2~3个数量级且需激活低秩权重路径；soft prompts则会增加序列位置。在检索重排序和领域分类任务中，当决策依赖于早期域内token时，偏移带来提升；在少样本推理等信号出现在后期的任务中结果不变。

Conclusion: 域条件位置偏移并非最强适配器，但提供了一种轻量级、可热切换的工具，适用于短文本的域内评分和校准。位置感知的prefill应用也有助于生成任务，而在每个缓存解码步骤都朴素应用则会导致重复。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models are least accurate at the beginning of a sequence, where little context forces reliance on a generic pretraining prior. We show that this cold-start penalty is domain dependent and reduce it with a domain-conditional position offset: a single learned vector added to the embedding activation at the first sequence positions while all model weights remain frozen. The offset trains in minutes on roughly one hundred documents, switches between domains without added sequence state, and has no measurable latency overhead. Across eight Mamba, GPT-NeoX, and Llama models spanning 410M to 8B parameters, it reduces held-out in-domain perplexity by up to 27%; the effect persists at 70B, and one position captures most of the benefit. A matched, converged direct logit-bias correction reaches at most only 7.9% and leaves later-token loss unchanged, showing that the offset propagates through model state rather than merely recalibrating the output prior. A tuned LoRA reaches lower perplexity but uses two to three orders of magnitude more parameters and an active low-rank weight path, while soft prompts add sequence positions. With wrong-domain controls, offsets improve retrieval reranking and domain classification when decisions depend on early in-domain tokens, For the few-shot reasoning whose signal occurs later, the results maintains unchanged. Position-aware prefill application also help generation tasks, whereas naive application at every cached decoding step causes repetition. The offset is therefore not the strongest adapter, but a lightweight, hot switchable tool for short in-domain scoring and calibration.

</details>


### [98] [TD-DPO: Difference-Aware Preference Optimization for Mitigating Sycophancy in Clinical Autism Intervention Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18304)
*Shuzhong Lai,Junhong Lai,Chenxi Li,Qing Zhou,Haifeng Li,Gang Pan,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MEDA策略和TD-DPO方法，通过最小编辑数据增强和标记级差异偏好优化，在减少大语言模型谄媚行为的同时保持自闭症干预对话能力，实现更优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自闭症儿童干预对话中的谄媚行为会增加安全风险，现有监督微调依赖正例难以识别和纠正失败模式，而序列级偏好优化会过度更新与偏好无关的token，损害干预能力。

Method: 提出最小编辑数据增强(MEDA)策略构建受控稳定的最小编辑偏好对，以及标记级差异直接偏好优化(TD-DPO)方法，对选择与拒绝响应间的差异token赋予更高权重，对共享token降低权重以抑制背景漂移。

Result: 在多个主干网络和评估器上的实验表明，TD-DPO在离线设置下实现了谄媚缓解与干预能力保持之间更优的权衡。

Conclusion: TD-DPO作为一种实用的对齐方法，在自闭症干预场景中具有潜力。

Abstract: The sycophancy of large language models can increase the safety risk in intervention dialogue for autistic children. Supervised fine-tuning can somewhat reduce sycophancy, but relying solely on positive examples is often insufficient to identify and correct failure patterns. We observe that sycophancy behaviors can often be localized to a limited span within the model response. In this regime, sequence-level preference optimization can over-update preference-irrelevant tokens and degrade intervention ability. To address this, we propose the \textbf{M}inimal \textbf{E}dit \textbf{D}ata \textbf{A}ugmentation (MEDA) strategy to construct controlled, stable, minimal edit preference pairs and \textbf{T}oken-level \textbf{D}ifference \textbf{D}irect \textbf{P}reference \textbf{O}ptimization (TD-DPO), which upweights difference tokens between chosen and rejected responses while downweighting shared tokens to suppress background drift. Extensive experiments across multiple backbones and evaluators show that TD-DPO achieves a better trade-off between sycophancy mitigation and intervention ability retention in our offline settings, highlighting its potential as a practical alignment approach for autism intervention.

</details>


### [99] [The Information Shadow: Measuring Structural Limits on What Language Models Can Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18305)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“信息阴影”（information shadow）概念，指语言模型从文本中学习时存在的三种结构性限制（表达不可达、统计不可识别、梯度不可达），并通过三种探针方法验证了这些限制并非数据覆盖不足，而是文本学习的固有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将语言模型的性能缺陷归因于数据覆盖不足或规模不够，但作者认为存在更深层的结构性限制——无论数据规模多大，仅从文本学习的模型都无法掌握某些现象。

Method: 提出三种探针：1) 语言压缩残差（Type I）：对比文本学习器与全信号学习器，证明差距是通道本身的属性；2) 反事实区分测试（Type II）：在数据与两条不相容规则都一致时，模型行为由归纳偏置决定，少量数据可双向引导；3) 盆地逃逸映射（Type III）：构造一个100%可表示但标准训练0%可达的函数，且宽度缩放无效。

Result: Type I：300倍数据差距仍保持平坦；Type II：反事实行为由归纳偏置决定，5%消歧数据可实现双向引导（r=±1.0, p<1e-10）；Type III：标准训练0%可达，邻近初始化立即成功，宽度缩放无效（p=1.6×10^-14）。三个效应均通过控制实验排除了容量或模态干扰。

Conclusion: 语言模型的知识存在结构性限制（信息阴影），这独立于数据规模。研究对基准设计、能力审计和不确定性估计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Some limits on what language models know are not gaps in data coverage but structural properties of learning from text. We introduce the information shadow: the region of phenomena that a text-trained learner cannot acquire regardless of scale, comprising (I) structures language cannot express, (II) functions that are statistically non-identifiable from the training distribution, and (III) functions that are representable but unreachable by gradient-based training. We give each type a probe that is decisive because the premise of the shadow is, in that setting, provable. For Type I, Language Compression Residuals compare a text learner, which sees only a lossy text-like encoding of the signal, against a full-signal learner, which sees the underlying signal directly. The text learner sits at a computable expressibility ceiling while the full-signal learner pulls away by a gap that stays flat across 300x more data, so the deficit is a property of the channel, not of training. For Type II, the Counterfactual Distinction Test trains models on data exactly consistent with two incompatible rules. Across a provable string task and a language-like agreement task, behavior on counterfactuals is set by the model's inductive bias, while 5% disambiguating data steers the learned rule bidirectionally to either target (r = +/-1.0, p < 1e-10). For Type III, Basin Escape Mapping exhibits a function that is representable at 100% (by hand construction) yet reached 0% of the time by standard training and instantly from a nearby initialization, with width scaling providing no help (p = 1.6 x 10^-14). Each effect is isolated by a control that rules out a capacity or modality artifact. We release the probe suite and discuss implications for benchmark design, capability auditing, and shadow-aware uncertainty.

</details>


### [100] [Gradient-Energy Guided Block-Wise Perturbations for Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18306)
*Zhen Huang,Jiaxin Deng,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEAR-SAM通过指数移动平均的块梯度平方作为敏感性信号，以闭式约束优化重新分配SAM的固定扰动预算，在保持全局半径不变且不增加额外计算开销的前提下，提升了跨架构和任务的泛化与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAM的扰动预算按小批量梯度范数分配，噪声大且无法反映参数块在整个训练过程中累积的敏感性，导致分配不优。

Method: 维护块梯度平方的指数移动平均（EMA）作为轻量曲率相关敏感性信号，通过闭式约束优化将固定的SAM预算重新分配到各参数块，保留全局SAM半径，无需Hessian-向量积或显式Fisher估计。

Result: 在图像分类、迁移学习、噪声标签学习等任务上，GEAR-SAM相比标准SAM实现了更好的泛化和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 固定的扰动预算应根据训练过程中功能网络块敏感性的演化进行动态重新分配，这为锐度感知优化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) improves generalization by minimizing the worst-case loss in a local parameter neighborhood. Standard SAM implicitly allocates its global perturbation budget across parameter blocks according to instantaneous minibatch gradient norms. Such an allocation can be noisy and may not reflect the sensitivity that blocks accumulate throughout training. We propose Gradient-Energy Adaptive Radius SAM (GEAR-SAM), which maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) of squared block gradients as a lightweight, curvature-related sensitivity signal and allocates the fixed SAM budget through a closed-form constrained optimization. GEAR-SAM preserves the global SAM radius, requires no Hessian-vector products or explicit Fisher estimation, and adds only scalar state beyond SAM. Experiments on image classification, transfer learning, noisy-label learning, and partition studies demonstrate improved generalization and robustness across architectures and tasks. More broadly, GEAR-SAM provides a dynamic view of sharpness-aware optimization: a fixed perturbation budget should be redistributed as the sensitivity of functional network blocks evolves during training.

</details>


### [101] [Agentic Calibration of Grey-Box Simulation Models: An LLM-Driven Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18308)
*David Gómez-Guillén,Mireia Diaz,Josep Lluis Arcos,Jesús Cerquides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种代理型校准方法，利用大型语言模型作为优化器，通过在系统提示中以自然语言形式纳入约束条件，实现对灰盒仿真模型的高效校准。与Nelder-Mead和贝叶斯优化相比，该方法在无需额外建模开销的情况下，以更少的模型评估次数实现了更优或相当的误差水平，主要代价是每轮迭代的推理时间增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰盒仿真模型的校准是一个约束优化问题，面临模型评估昂贵、参数空间高维、需满足可解释性约束等挑战。现有方法中，经典优化器（如NM）样本效率低，尤其在约束条件下表现不佳；贝叶斯优化虽能减少评估次数，但需要复杂的建模机制来处理约束。因此，需要一种既样本高效又易于处理约束的校准方法。

Method: 提出代理型校准方法（Agentic Calibration），核心思想是将大型语言模型（LLM）作为优化器。约束条件以纯自然语言形式写入系统提示（system prompt）中，无需额外的数学建模。LLM基于当前参数配置和仿真结果，自主决策下一步参数调整方向，迭代搜索最优解。

Result: 在肛门癌仿真模型上进行实验：无约束校准下，代理方法的最佳误差显著低于NM和BO，且所需模型评估次数更少；有约束校准下，代理方法与BO误差水平相当，两者均优于NM。代价是每轮迭代的推理时间增加。

Conclusion: 代理型校准方法能以更少的模型评估次数取得有竞争力的性能，约束处理几乎零额外成本（仅需文本描述）。主要权衡在于每轮推理成本增加，因此尤其适用于仿真时间占主导的场景。此外，每轮迭代的推理过程具有可审计性和可解释性，便于决策审查和向第三方解释。

Abstract: Calibration of grey-box simulation models is a constrained optimization problem in which model evaluations are expensive, the parameter space can be high-dimensional, and the search must respect plausibility constraints. Although the simulation code is fully available to the analyst, the joint effect of multiple parameters remains difficult to predict analytically. Classical optimizers such as Nelder--Mead (NM) are simple to deploy but sample-inefficient, particularly under constraints. Modern Bayesian Optimization methods achieve competitive solutions with far fewer evaluations but require non-trivial modeling machinery for constraint handling. We introduce an agentic calibration method in which a large language model acts as the optimizer, with constraints incorporated as a plain-language section of the system prompt. We evaluate the agentic method, NM, and Bayesian Optimization (BO) on an anal cancer simulation model under both unconstrained and clinically constrained calibration. Under unconstrained calibration, the agentic method achieves substantially lower best error than BO and NM, while requiring fewer model evaluations. Under constrained calibration, the agentic method reaches comparable error levels and both outperform NM. These results are obtained at the cost of increased inference time per iteration. Agentic calibration achieves competitive performance with substantially fewer model evaluations, and constraint handling is essentially free at the modeller-facing interface through simple textual specifications rather than additional modelling machinery. The main trade-off lies in increased per-iteration inference cost, making the approach particularly suitable when simulation time dominates. Beyond performance, the per-iteration rationale makes the search auditable and explainable, so its decisions can be scrutinised and justified to third parties.

</details>


### [102] [Spatio-Temporal Prediction of Unsteady Airfoil Aerodynamics Using Augmented Graph Neural Ordinary Differential Equations with Exogenous Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18309)
*Henrik Lange,Reik Thormann,Philipp Bekemeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GNODE方法，将图神经网络与神经常微分方程相结合，用于预测俯仰翼型的非定常气动力。相比自回归GNN基线，GNODE在时间稳定性、空间平滑性和预测精度上均表现更优，适用于含跨音速激波、瞬态行为等非线性的时空系统建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CFD方法（如URANS、线性频域法）计算成本高或受线性假设限制，而机器学习代理模型虽能快速计算非线性关系，但自回归GNN存在误差累积导致时间滚动不稳定。因此需要一种兼具计算效率和时间稳定性的方法。

Method: 将图神经网络（GNN）与增强型神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）相结合，提出GNODE（Graph Neural Ordinary Differential Equations）框架。在离散化空间域上操作，通过额外潜在维度增强模型表达能力以捕捉历史效应，实现非自回归的时间预测。

Result: 在俯仰翼型数据集（含跨音速激波、瞬态行为和动态非线性）上，GNODE相比自回归GNN基线在时间稳定性、空间平滑性和整体精度上均更优；增强潜在维度提升了模型的表达能力和准确度。

Conclusion: GNODE方法能够有效建模具有外源输入的非线性时空系统，为解决气动预测中的误差累积问题提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Unsteady aerodynamic phenomena, such as gusts, turbulence, and fluid-structure interactions affect an aircraft during flight. For design, optimisation and certification, it is indispensable to quantify such unsteady aerodynamic effects. Industry-standard computational fluid dynamics methods, such as solving the unsteady Reynolds-averaged Navier-Stokes equations or the linearized frequency domain method, are either computationally expensive or restricted by assumptions like linearity. Once trained, machine learning methods are capable of computing non-linear relationships very fast, making them suitable as surrogate models. By autoregressively applying graph neural networks (GNNs), operating on a discretised spatial domain, spatio-temporal predictions can be made. However, autoregressive GNNs suffer from error accumulation leading to unstable rollouts over time. Here we show that combining GNNs with augmented Neural Ordinary Differential Equations yields temporally stable predictions of the surface forces on a pitching airfoil. We found that our approach, called GNODE, based on Graph Neural Ordinary Differential Equations, provides temporally more stable, spatially smoother, and overall more accurate results than an autoregressive GNN baseline. Tests are conducted on a dataset consisting of a simulations of a pitching airfoil, including transonic shocks, transient behaviour and dynamic non-linearities. Augmenting GNODEs with additional latent dimensions improves the expressivity and accuracy by capturing underlying history effects. The developed method demonstrates an approach that is suitable to model non-linear spatio-temporal systems with exogenous inputs.

</details>


### [103] [Interactive Training 2: Auditable Control Plane for Live Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18314)
*Wentao Zhang,Xuanhe Pan,Han Zhou,Yang Lu,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Interactive Training 2，一个通过共享协议控制训练流程的开源控制平面，支持人类和自动化控制器在安全控制点提交请求并验证执行，已在五个NLP和强化学习工作流中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验跟踪器能展示训练进度，但修改正在运行的训练任务仍需编写训练器特定的代码，缺乏统一的、可交互的控制接口。

Method: 设计一个基于共享协议的开源控制平面，训练应用声明暴露的设置和动作，人类和自动化控制器通过统一接口提交请求，训练循环在安全控制点验证并应用这些请求。结合Aim工作区展示实时指标、控制项及请求与结果的时序记录。

Result: 在五个NLP和强化学习工作流上成功演示了系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 发布的代码和轨迹为可审计的、人类或智能体引导的训练提供了可复用的基础。

Abstract: Experiment trackers show how training is progressing, but changing a live run still usually requires trainer-specific code. We present Interactive Training 2, an open-source control plane for steering training through a shared protocol. Training applications declare which settings and actions they expose, humans and automated controllers submit requests through the same interface, and the training loop validates and applies them at safe control points. A customized Aim workspace combines live metrics and controls with a chronological record of requests and outcomes. We demonstrate the system across five NLP and reinforcement-learning workflows. The released code and traces provide a reusable foundation for auditable human- and agent-guided training.

</details>


### [104] [Cost Accounting for Reactive Computational Graphs: Exhaustive Sweeps, Sequential Mutation, and the Backward-Locality Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18323)
*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在反应式计算图引擎上，严格分析了神经网络的穷举站点干预（如激活补丁扫描、电路发现等）的加速比和成本。证明加速比并非通用常数（介于1到2之间，均匀深度时才为2），推导了序列突变和批量突变的精确成本公式，并验证了反向传播下加速比坍缩为1。所有理论在NeuroDSL引擎上得到零误差验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的穷举站点干预（如激活补丁扫描、电路发现、系统消融研究）成本高昂，其主导因素是每次突变后的重计算。现有工作缺乏对这种计算成本的精确理论分析，尤其是考虑不同深度权重分布、突变序列顺序等实际因素时的加速比和开销规律。

Method: 基于反应式计算图引擎（其无效化传播精确覆盖下游锥），对三种工作负载进行严格成本核算：（1）穷举扫描：推导加速比与深度权重分布的Karamata指数的关系；（2）序列持久突变：推导序列成本超出孤立成本之和的精确超计数，以及封闭形式的极值；（3）批量突变：证明顺序无关性和次可加性，成本为各站点锥的并集加新增节点。同时证明反向传播下前向局部性的精确镜像导致加速比坍缩为1。

Result: （1）穷举扫描加速比收敛到(q+2)/(q+1)（权重靠近输出端）或q+2（靠近输入端），均匀深度时为2；时钟上限约1.79。（2）序列突变间插成本有精确超计数公式，不同插入顺序有封闭极值；批量应用成本为站点锥并集加新增节点，与顺序无关。（3）反向传播下加速比坍缩为1。所有公式在NeuroDSL上以零误差验证。

Conclusion: 反应式计算图引擎上的穷举干预加速比受深度权重分布显著影响且存在上界（<2），序列插入顺序影响总成本，批量应用具有次可加性，反向传播场景下无加速优势。这些理论为神经网络可解释性研究中的计算优化提供了精确指导。

Abstract: Exhaustive site-by-site interventions on a neural network's computational graph -- activation-patching sweeps, circuit-discovery searches, systematic ablation studies -- mutate the graph at every candidate site, and their cost is dominated by recomputation after each mutation. On a reactive graph engine whose invalidation provably touches exactly the downstream cone of a mutated node, we give a complete cost accounting for such workloads. First, the aggregate speedup of an exhaustive sweep over independent full recomputations is not a universal constant: if per-layer weight varies regularly with depth at Karamata index q, the ratio converges to (q+2)/(q+1) when weight concentrates near the output and to q+2 near the input, recovering 2 only in the depth-uniform case; a wall-clock corollary predicts a ceiling of about 1.79, below 2, until interpreter overhead is compiled away. Second, we prove the exact cost of a sequence of persistent mutations, never undone between insertions: the interleaved cost exceeds the isolated sum by an exact overcount summed over comparable site pairs, with closed-form extremes over insertion orders, while batched application is order-independent and sub-additive, costing exactly the union of the sites' cones plus the fresh nodes. Third, we prove the exact mirror of forward locality for the backward pass, showing it collapses the aggregate speedup to 1 under backpropagation on architectures without long skip connections. Every identity is validated on NeuroDSL, a reactive graph engine in Julia: measured sweep ratios converge to the predicted limits under four cost profiles; the training-mode ratio collapses to 1 at the predicted rate; and all 18 per-graft sequential costs and the batched total match the closed forms at zero tolerance across three insertion orders.

</details>


### [105] [Dynamic Loss Balancing for Joint SOH and RUL Prediction of Lithium-Ion Batteries via a Rotary SOH-Injected Prior Battery Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18329)
*Shuhao Chen,Tianyu Shi,Yiwen Huang,Chengyi Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoSIP-Batt，一种基于贝叶斯多任务学习的统一框架，通过同方差不确定性加权、解耦双分类token、逐维度门控融合、梯度分离操作和旋转位置编码（RoPE），联合估计锂离子电池的健康状态（SOH）和剩余使用寿命（RUL），在NASA、MIT-Stanford和HUST数据集上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多任务学习框架无法平衡SOH估计的有界低方差噪声与RUL长期预测的无界非线性膨胀不确定性，任务异方差性严重阻碍了SOH和RUL的联合预测。

Method: 将联合预测建模为贝叶斯多任务目标，引入同方差不确定性加权机制动态缩放任务梯度；采用解耦双分类token和逐维度门控融合机制，辅以梯度分离操作防止高方差RUL更新破坏SOH表征空间；在共享Transformer骨干中引入RoPE实现平移不变的相对时间建模；将中间SOH估计作为物理退化先验直接注入RUL回归头。

Result: 在NASA数据集上SOH估计MAE降至1.994%，在Stanford数据集上RUL预测误差限制在62.85 cycles，显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: RoSIP-Batt是一种高度泛化、计算高效的解决方案，适用于实时嵌入式电池管理系统部署。

Abstract: The deployment of reliable lithium-ion battery management systems is crucial for accelerating electrification, yet the joint prognosis of State of Health (SOH) and Remaining Useful Life (RUL) remains severely hindered by task heteroscedasticity. Conventional multi-task learning frameworks fail to balance the bounded, low-variance noise of SOH estimation with the unbounded, nonlinearly expanding uncertainty of long-term RUL predictions. Here, we present the Rotary SOH-Injected Prior Battery Transformer (RoSIP-Batt), a unified co-estimation framework that resolves these optimization conflicts. By formulating joint prediction as a Bayesian multi-task objective, RoSIP-Batt introduces a homoscedastic uncertainty weighting mechanism to dynamically scale task-specific gradients based on learned residual noise levels. The architecture leverages decoupled dual classification tokens and a per-dimension gated fusion mechanism, secured by a gradient-detachment operator to prevent high-variance RUL updates from corrupting the stable SOH representation space. To capture electrochemical degradation patterns without relying on absolute cycle steps, Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) is incorporated into a shared Transformer backbone to model translation-invariant relative temporal profiles. Crucially, the intermediate SOH estimate is directly injected into the RUL regression head as a physical degradation prior. Evaluations across the NASA, MIT-Stanford, and HUST datasets show that RoSIP-Batt significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing SOH estimation error to 1.994% MAE on NASA and restricting RUL prediction error to 62.85 cycles on Stanford. These findings establish RoSIP-Batt as a highly generalizable, computationally efficient solution suitable for real-time embedded BMS deployment.

</details>


### [106] [Physics-Guided Masked Multi-Task Network for Edge-Friendly Battery Health Diagnostics from Sto-chastically Fragmented Charging Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18330)
*Shuhao Chen,Tianyu Shi,Chengyi Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoSIP-Batt，一种基于贝叶斯多任务学习的统一框架，通过同方差不确定性加权、解耦双分类令牌、门控融合机制和旋转位置编码，联合估计锂离子电池的健康状态（SOH）和剩余使用寿命（RUL），有效解决了任务异方差性导致的优化冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多任务学习框架难以平衡SOH估计的有界低方差噪声与RUL长期预测的无界非线性扩展不确定性，任务异方差性严重阻碍了SOH与RUL的联合预测。

Method: 1) 贝叶斯多目标框架下引入同方差不确定性加权机制，动态缩放任务特定梯度；2) 解耦双分类令牌与逐维度门控融合机制，配合梯度分离算子防止RUL高方差更新破坏SOH表示空间；3) 共享Transformer骨干中采用旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）建模平移不变的相对时间轮廓；4) 将中间SOH估计作为物理退化先验直接注入RUL回归头。

Result: 在NASA、MIT-Stanford和HUST数据集上，RoSIP-Batt显著优于现有基线：NASA数据集上SOH估计MAE降至1.994%，Stanford数据集上RUL预测误差限制在62.85个周期。

Conclusion: RoSIP-Batt是一种高度泛化、计算高效的解决方案，适用于实时嵌入式电池管理系统（BMS）部署。
