</details>


### [176] [One Model, Many Graphs: Learning over Attributed Graphs across Heterogeneous Modalities with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19128)
*Jiayi Yang,Yifang Chen,Yuanfu Sun,Jiajin Liu,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMG-VLM提出了一种统一的框架，利用预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为共享骨干网络，通过结构感知的图适配器处理具有文本属性、图像属性或多模态属性的图数据，在节点分类和链接预测任务上优于现有GNN和LLM基线方法，并展现出良好的跨图泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图学习方法通常针对固定的模态模式设计，不同模态设置（纯文本属性、纯视觉属性、两者兼有）需要单独建模，限制了可扩展性和跨图泛化能力。VLM虽能为文本和视觉信息提供统一表征空间，但其作为图结构数据的通用骨干网络的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: OMG-VLM采用预训练VLM作为共享骨干网络，并引入结构感知的图适配器，该适配器在整合邻域结构信息的同时保持与VLM原生嵌入空间的兼容性，从而在单一模型下有效处理文本属性图、图像属性图和多模态属性图。

Result: 在多个领域的实验表明，OMG-VLM在节点分类和链接预测等属性图学习任务上持续优于最先进的GNN和LLM基线方法，并对未见过的图及不同模态模式展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: OMG-VLM验证了VLM作为统一骨干网络处理异构模态属性图的可行性和有效性，为跨模态、跨图的图学习提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a unified representation space for textual and visual information, yet their potential as general-purpose backbones for graph-structured data remains largely unexplored. In practice, attributed graphs exhibit substantial modality heterogeneity: some graphs contain only textual node attributes, others only visual attributes, while still others provide both. Existing graph learning approaches are typically designed for fixed modality schemas, requiring separate models for different settings and limiting scalability and cross-graph generalization. To bridge this gap, we present OMG-VLM (One Model, Many Graphs with Vision-Language Models), a unified framework for learning over attributed graphs across heterogeneous modality schemas. OMG-VLM leverages a pretrained VLM as a shared backbone and introduces structure-aware graph adapters that integrate neighborhood information while remaining compatible with the VLM's native embedding space. This design enables effective learning over text-attributed, image-attributed, and multi-attributed graphs within a single model. Extensive experiments across diverse domains show that OMG-VLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art GNN- and LLM-based baselines on attributed graph learning tasks such as node classification and link prediction, while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen graphs and varying modality schemas. The source code is available at https://github.com/Jo-eyang/OMG-VLM.

</details>


### [177] [Incomplete Observations Boost Evolutionary Performance in Ocean Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19147)
*Yangyang Kong,Yutong Jiang,Yanhai Gan,Junyu Dong,Feng Gao,Xiaopei Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式状态空间模型与优化框架，能够直接从稀疏、含噪声的观测数据中学习海洋动态，无需依赖完整的再分析数据集，实现了高保真重建与准确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的海洋建模方法严重依赖完整的再分析数据集，这带来了计算约束，且模型性能受限于训练数据的质量。因此，需要一种能够直接从稀疏、不完整的真实观测中学习的方法。

Method: 将海洋物理量视为隐藏状态、观测数据视为观测值，构建连续状态空间的隐马尔可夫模型。初始状态和状态转移模块用神经网络实现，发射模块采用掩码高斯分布。基于期望最大化算法推导优化框架，交替使用 Langevin 动力学重建高保真海洋场与优化深度神经网络。

Result: 在 CMIP6 模拟数据和 FY-3D 卫星数据上的实验表明，该方法实现了高保真重建和准确预测，稀疏观测能够直接改进模型对海洋状态动力学的表征。

Conclusion: 该工作为下一代地球系统模型直接从稀疏、不完整的真实世界观测中学习提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Data-driven methods have revolutionized ocean modeling, yet current approaches rely heavily on complete reanalysis datasets, imposing computational constraints and limiting model performance to that of the training data. Here, we present a generative state-space model and an optimization framework that enable learning directly from sparse and noisy observations. The model is essentially a hidden Markov model with a continuous state space, where oceanic physical quantities are treated as hidden states and measurements as observations, enabling a unified representation of ocean fields and observational data. Both the initial-state and state-transition modules are implemented as neural networks to capture the complexity and temporal evolution of ocean states, while the emission module is formulated as a masked Gaussian distribution. To train the model from sparse observations, we derive an optimization framework based on the expectation-maximization (EM) algorithm. The framework alternately reconstructs high-fidelity ocean fields via Langevin dynamics and optimizes deep neural networks to capture temporal evolution. Theoretical analysis shows that the framework maximizes the likelihood of observations under the generative model. For efficiency, we assume that ocean-state evolution follows a stationary, ergodic, and Markovian stochastic process and adopt only length-two state sequences during optimization. Experiments on CMIP6 simulation data and FY-3D satellite data demonstrate high-fidelity reconstruction and accurate prediction, showing that sparse observations can directly improve the model's representation of ocean-state dynamics. This work offers a scalable pathway for next-generation Earth system models to learn directly from sparse, incomplete real-world observations.

</details>


### [178] [Breaking the Homogeneity Assumption: Specialized Multi-Generator Adversarial Learning for Rare Failure Detection in Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19153)
*Alexis Lazanas,Georgios Kampouropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向故障子类型的多生成器GAN架构，用于解决预测性维护中高度不平衡且异构的工业数据问题，实验表明该方法相比传统重采样和单生成器GAN能生成更真实的少数类样本，在PR-AUC和召回率上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不平衡处理方法（欠采样、SMOTE、代价敏感学习）假设少数类样本是同质的，但工业故障数据往往包含多种故障模式（异构、多模态分布），导致这些方法效果受限。因此需要一种考虑故障子类型差异的生成式增强方法。

Method: 提出一种多生成器GAN架构，每个生成器独立学习一种故障子类型；采用防泄漏的实验设计；在AI4I 2020数据集上与代价敏感学习、随机欠采样、SMOTE过采样、单生成器GAN进行对比；使用PR-AUC作为主要评估指标。

Result: 多生成器GAN框架生成了更真实的少数类样本，在PR-AUC和召回率上均优于传统重采样方法和单生成器GAN增强方法。

Conclusion: 考虑故障子类型差异的生成式增强方法能有效提升预测性维护系统中对罕见故障的识别能力，为工业场景下的不平衡学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Supervised learning models in the predictive maintenance field are regularly trained on highly imbalanced industrial datasets: machine failures occur rarely but have a disproportionate effect on operations. In addition to the clear class disparity, failure data are typically non-homogeneous, with different failure modes arising from distinct physical processes and exhibiting a multimodal distribution across minorities and classes. Traditional imbalance-management methods, e.g., undersampling, SMOTE-based interpolation, or cost-sensitive learning, typically assume that the minority population is homogeneous. This means their effectiveness is severely limited in the multifaceted conditions encountered in industrial practice. This paper determines the possibility of a failure-type-conscious generative augmentation program to improve the identification of infrequent failures in predictive maintenance systems. An experimental design that is leakage-safe is used to compare five imbalance-handling methods: cost-sensitive learning, random undersampling, SMOTE oversampling, single-generator GAN augmentation, and a specialized multi-generator GAN architecture that has independent generators that are asked to learn individual failure subtypes. Precision/Recall-oriented measures are used to quantify model performance; the main evaluation measure is the PR-AUC. Experiments conducted on the AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance dataset indicate that the proposed multi-generator GAN framework produces more realistic minority samples, yielding higher PR-AUC and recall scores compared to traditional resampling methods and individual-generator GAN augmentation.

</details>


### [179] [Neural Kolmogorov Equations: Parallelizable Learning of Stochastic Dynamics under General Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19173)
*Arthur Bizzi,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经Kolmogorov方程（NKEs），通过将神经SDE的学习问题从建模随机轨迹重构成建模概率密度演化，实现对一般Lévy型随机驱动的学习，并利用拉格朗日Galerkin投影和算子分裂实现时间并行训练，在多个随机基准上验证了其预测准确性和训练效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经SDE方法假设噪声为解耦连续的，难以建模实际场景中的耦合噪声和跳跃过程等复杂随机驱动，且自回归训练方式在长时间尺度上计算效率低下。

Method: 基于Kolmogorov前向方程将神经SDE转化为确定性无穷维问题，通过算子结构学习一般Lévy型随机强迫，采用拉格朗日Galerkin投影和算子分裂实现时间并行训练。

Result: 在包含耦合噪声和跳跃过程的多个随机基准上，NKEs准确恢复了确定性和随机动力学，预测精度有竞争力，训练效率更高。

Conclusion: NKEs为学习复杂随机动力学提供了一种灵活、高效的框架，克服了传统神经SDE在噪声类型和训练效率方面的局限性。

Abstract: Neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have emerged as powerful tools for learning noisy or stochastic dynamics directly from data; however, existing approaches largely assume uncoupled and continuous noise, limiting their applicability to realistic stochastic drivers, and often scale poorly in time, requiring expensive autoregressive training. To address these limitations, we propose Neural Kolmogorov Equations (NKEs), a deterministic, infinite-dimensional reformulation of Neural SDEs based on the Kolmogorov Forward equation, transforming the learning problem from modelling individual stochastic trajectories to modelling the evolution of probability densities. NKEs learn general Lévy-type stochastic forcing directly through the operator structure of the KFE, and enable parallel-in-time training via a Lagrangian Galerkin projection and operator splitting. We evaluate NKEs on several stochastic benchmarks, including systems with coupled noise and jump processes, and verify that NKEs provide flexible models that accurately recover deterministic and stochastic dynamics with competitive predictive accuracy and improved training efficiency. Code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>


### [180] [Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization for Offline RL Under Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19199)
*Li-Rong Zhou,Qin-Wen Luo,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE（基于不确定性估计的保守查询与自适应正则化框架），通过Morse网络估计策略动作的不确定性，并据此设计保守查询策略和不确定性感知的自适应正则化，以改进离线强化学习中偏好查询的选择与利用，在D4RL基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习方法在利用人类偏好反馈时面临两大挑战：选择信息量大的偏好查询以及有效利用收集到的反馈。当前方法仅依赖策略动作与数据集动作之间的距离进行查询选择，并施加固定约束使策略贴近查询偏好，导致策略更新不稳定且与价值正则化结合效果差。

Method: 提出CARE框架：1) 使用Morse网络估计策略动作相对于离线数据集的不确定性；2) 基于不确定性估计，设计保守查询策略，选择性查询靠近数据集的动作以保持Bellman更新的稳定性；3) 提出不确定性感知的自适应正则化机制，在策略优化过程中动态调整数据级约束。

Result: 将CARE框架与CQL集成，在D4RL基准上进行广泛评估，在多种任务中展现出优越或具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性估计同时优化偏好查询的选择与利用，能有效提升离线强化学习的性能，克服了固定约束策略带来的不稳定问题。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn an effective policy from a static dataset, but its performance is fundamentally limited by dataset coverage. Action preference queries leverage expert feedback without additional environment interaction, enabling policy improvement during offline training. However, existing methods still face two key challenges: selecting informative preference queries and effectively exploiting the collected feedback. Current approaches typically rely only on the distance between policy actions and dataset actions for query selection, while enforcing fixed constraints that keep the policy close to queried preferences. Such strategies often lead to unstable policy updates and integrate poorly with value regularization. To address these limitations, we propose Conservative Query and Adaptive Regularization under Uncertainty Estimation, a lightweight framework that jointly improves preference querying and preference exploitation. Specifically, we employ a Morse network to estimate the uncertainty of policy actions with respect to the offline dataset. Based on this uncertainty, we introduce a conservative query strategy that selectively queries actions near the dataset to preserve Bellman-update stability, together with an uncertainty-aware adaptive regularization scheme that dynamically adjusts data-level constraints during policy optimization. We integrate our framework with CQL and evaluate it extensively on the D4RL benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate superior or competitive performance across a wide range of tasks.

</details>


### [181] [AdaFlash: Adaptive Speculative Decoding via On-Policy Distilled Diffusion Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19223)
*Yu-Yang Qian,Hao-Cong Wu,Chen Chen,Jiacheng Sun,Zhenhua Dong,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散草稿模型的双向注意力机制存在领域级和令牌级的高方差问题，并提出AdaFlash框架，通过基于反向KL散度的同策略蒸馏算法降低领域级方差，以及自适应长度头动态调整候选序列长度以减少令牌级方差，在高效并发场景下吞吐量提升高达66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散草稿模型（如DFlash）利用双向注意力机制实现了并行生成和全局上下文建模，但双向注意力是一把双刃剑：它同时引入了高方差问题——不同领域间接受率波动大（领域级），以及不同令牌位置的质量异质性（令牌级），这严重影响了推测解码的效率。

Method: 提出AdaFlash框架，包含两个组件：(i) 针对扩散草稿模型设计的基于反向KL散度的同策略蒸馏算法（OPD），实现稳定收敛并有效降低领域级方差；(ii) 自适应长度头（Adaptive Length Head），可动态调整候选序列长度，降低目标模型的验证开销并处理令牌级方差。

Result: AdaFlash在部署过程中持续提升加速比，在高并发场景下效果尤为显著，相比之前最先进的方法吞吐量提升高达约66%。

Conclusion: 通过针对性地解决扩散草稿模型双向注意力带来的领域级和令牌级方差问题，AdaFlash有效提升了推测解码的效率，尤其在推理服务的高并发场景中表现出色。

Abstract: Speculative decoding, in which a lightweight draft model first generates a draft sequence that is then verified in parallel by the target model, has become a prevalent paradigm for accelerating large language model inference. Recent work such as DFlash further boosts drafting efficiency by leveraging diffusion drafters, whose parallel denoising mechanism enables draft generation in a single forward pass. In this work, we uncover a central pitfall of diffusion drafters: bidirectional attention is a double-edged sword. On one hand, it endows the model with parallel generation and global contextual modeling capabilities; on the other hand, this inherent global dependency introduces high variance at both the domain-level and the token-level: acceptance rates fluctuate substantially across different domains, and draft token quality also varies heterogeneously at different token positions. To tackle this issue, we propose AdaFlash framework, comprising two components: (i) an on-policy distillation (OPD) algorithm with reverse-KL divergence tailored for diffusion drafters, bringing stable convergence and effectively reducing domain-level variance; and (ii) an adaptive length head that dynamically adjusts the candidate sequence length on the fly, substantially lowering the verification cost of the target model and handling token-level variance. Experiments demonstrate that AdaFlash consistently improves speedup rate during deployment, with especially significant gains in high-concurrency scenarios, achieving up to approximately 66% higher throughput than previous state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [182] [S3: Stable Subgoal Selection by Constraining Uncertainty of Coarse Dynamics in Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19232)
*Kshitij Kumar Srivastava,Kshitij Jerath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于粗粒度动力学的内在动机方法（Coarse Dynamics-Aware Intrinsic Motivation），通过为高层智能体提供动力学感知的密集奖励信号，使其在非平稳长时域任务中更策略性地选择子目标，从而提升层次强化学习（HRL）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在HRL中，高层智能体接收的环境奖励稀疏且延迟，同时其性能高度依赖低层执行能力；现有方法缺乏对高层子目标选择的战略性引导机制。

Method: 利用粗粒度动力学（跨多步的聚合环境转移）建模高层智能体操作的时间尺度上的预测不确定性，通过混合密度网络（MDN）近似不同离散度度量来量化不确定性，并将此不确定性最小化作为内在奖励，驱动高层智能体选择风险规避的子目标。

Result: 在非平稳长时域环境中，所提方法优于当前最先进的HRL方法，验证了密集的动力学感知内在奖励能有效引导高层策略的风险规避行为。

Conclusion: 粗粒度动力学感知的内在动机为高层智能体提供了稳定的策略学习信号和导航结构，是解决HRL中高层反馈稀疏问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) intends to separate strategic planning from primitive execution. It has been widely successful in solving long-horizon and complex tasks, where flat-RL algorithms have difficulty in learning. However, while the low-level agent in HRL benefits from dense feedback and abundant trial opportunities, the high-level agent receives sparse, delayed feedback from the environment and its performance depends on the low-level execution capability. In this paper, we study whether subgoal selection by the high-level agent can be performed more strategically, by providing it with dynamics-aware intrinsic motivation. Since motivation based on primitive transition dynamics would require broad coverage of the state-action space, we propose to use coarse dynamics, i.e., environment transitions aggregated over multiple steps at the temporal scale at which the high-level agent operates. This approach stabilizes the high-level policy by learning to minimize the predictive uncertainty associated with the coarse dynamics, and provides a guided structure for navigation. We model the predictive uncertainty by evaluating different dispersion metrics as approximated by a Mixture Density Network (MDN). Empirically, we observe that a dense, dynamics-aware intrinsic reward leads to risk-averse subgoal selection, enabling it to outperform state-of-the-art HRL methods in non-stationary long-horizon environments.

</details>


### [183] [In-Context Time Series Classification with Random Convolutional Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19234)
*Joscha Cüppers,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MASHT方法，将随机卷积特征（MultiRocket和Hydra）与预训练表格基础模型结合，无需任务特定训练即可进行时间序列分类，在单变量任务上达到SOTA水平，在多元任务上保持强竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的随机卷积变换虽能高效提取时间序列特征，但仅搭配简单的线性分类器，未能充分利用这些丰富表示。研究者探究预训练表格基础模型是否能更有效地利用这些特征。

Method: 提出MASHT流水线，将MultiRocket和Hydra特征提取器与预训练表格基础模型相结合。仅需特征提取和直接推理，完全绕过特定任务的模型训练。

Result: 在单变量时间序列分类任务上，MASHT达到了与最先进基线方法相当的性能，平均排名优于HIVE-COTE 2.0。在多元数据集上，MASHT与最强参考方法相比具有高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 预训练表格基础模型能够有效利用随机卷积变换提取的时间序列特征，MASHT作为一种无需任务特定训练的框架，在时间序列分类任务上表现出色。

Abstract: Time series classification is central to domains like medical signal analysis, industrial monitoring, and sensor-based activity recognition, where class information manifests as localized shapes, specific frequencies, temporal shifts, or complex cross-channel interactions. Random convolutional transforms efficiently map these sequences to fixed-dimensional tabular features but are traditionally paired with simple linear classifiers. We investigate whether a pretrained tabular foundation model can more effectively harness these rich representations.
  We propose MASHT, a pipeline that marries MultiRocket and Hydra features with the power of in-context tabular foundation models. By leveraging a pretrained tabular foundation model, our approach completely bypasses task-specific model training, requiring only feature extraction and direct inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MASHT matches state-of-the-art time series classification baselines on univariate tasks, achieving a lower average rank than HIVE-COTE 2.0. On multivariate datasets, MASHT remains highly competitive with the strongest reference methods.

</details>


### [184] [DBMol: Design of High-Affinity, Target-Specific Small Molecules through Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19237)
*Yiming Qin,Kai Yi,Miruna Cretu,Sjors H. W. Scheres,Pietro Liò,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DBMol是一个基于结构预测模型（如Boltz-2和AlphaFold-3）的框架，通过交替优化和投影过程实现从零开始的小分子设计，能够生成具有高预测亲和力和特异性的分子，并在独立评估指标上表现出竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计高亲和力的小分子配体是药物发现的核心目标。近期结构预测模型（如AlphaFold-3和Boltz-2）在生物分子相互作用预测上取得突破，有望作为基础模型用于下游任务如结合亲和力预测。作者希望利用这些模型来指导从头小分子设计。

Method: DBMol采用交替优化-投影框架：优化阶段从初始分子出发，利用梯度优化改进口袋特异性相互作用和结构预测模型评估的结合亲和力；投影阶段通过流匹配模型将优化后的分子图映射到离散且化学有效的分子上。

Result: DBMol有效优化了Boltz-2亲和力代理指标，生成分子在Boltz-2评估下具有强预测亲和力和特异性。在独立评估（包括AF3）中，DBMol显著提高了口袋覆盖率，同时保持分子多样性，且在无参考配体监督条件下与无条件生成相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结构预测模型可作为从头分子设计的有效优化信号，DBMol方法展示了这一范式的可行性。

Abstract: Designing small molecule ligands that bind with high affinity to specific protein pockets is a fundamental goal in drug discovery, as small molecules constitute a major fraction of approved therapeutics. Recent breakthroughs in structure prediction, such as AlphaFold-3 and Boltz-2, enable accurate biomolecular interaction prediction and show promise as foundation models for downstream tasks, including binding affinity prediction. We propose to leverage these models and introduce DBMol, a new structure predictor-guided framework for de novo small molecule design. DBMol formulates an alternating optimization and projection process. In the optimization stage, DBMol starts from an initial molecule and uses gradient-based optimization to improve pocket-specific interactions and predicted binding affinity using a structure prediction model. In the projection stage, a flow-matching model maps the optimized molecular graph to discrete and chemically valid molecules. Experiments show that DBMol effectively optimizes the Boltz-2 affinity proxy and generates molecules with strong predicted affinity and specificity under Boltz-2 evaluation. To reduce self-confirmation bias, we further evaluate generated molecules using held-out metrics, including AF3-based evaluation. DBMol substantially improves pocket coverage while maintaining molecular diversity over unconditional generation, and is competitive under held-out metrics despite the absence of reference-ligand supervision. These results support the promise of structure prediction models as effective optimization signals for de novo molecular design.

</details>


### [185] [Thermodynamics-Informed Input Reparameterization for Neural Prediction of Real-Fluid Thermodynamic Properties in Supercritical Combustion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19241)
*Haoze Zhang,Han Li,Ke Xiao,Yangchen Xu,Runze Mao,Zhi X. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出目标对齐输入重参数化（TAIR）策略，通过将神经网络输入从原始焓替换为与目标匹配的热力学坐标（如理想气体温度估计和密度估计），引导网络学习实际流体与理想气体的偏差，从而在超临界燃烧模拟中显著提升温度、密度和可压缩系数的预测精度，RMSE降低约1.5-7.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超临界燃烧模拟中，基于焓的压力校正公式需要反复进行真实流体热力学性质评估（温度T、密度ρ、可压缩系数ψ），计算成本高昂。虽然神经网络代理可提供固定成本推理，但直接从(h,p,Y)映射到(T,ρ,ψ)需要捕捉复杂的焓-温关系和非理想状态方程响应，回归问题困难。

Method: 提出目标对齐输入重参数化（TAIR）：对温度网络，用常比热理想气体混合物焓近似反推出的温度估计替换原始焓坐标；对密度和可压缩系数网络，用理想气体密度估计替换。这些变换仅使用求解器可用变量和物种常数，使网络学习真实流体偏离理想基线的差值，而非从原始焓重构完整闭包。

Result: 在超临界甲烷-氧对冲火焰数据上，TAIR相比原始输入基线，温度、密度和可压缩系数的留出集RMSE分别降低约1.5倍、2.0倍和7.5倍；在未见过的应变率火焰（扩展热力学包络内）上，对应降幅达3.6倍、14.5倍和6.0倍。目标不一致的交叉重参数化控制表现更差，验证了增益源于热力学匹配的输入设计。

Conclusion: TAIR通过热力学启发的输入重参数化，有效简化了神经网络的学习任务，显著提升了超临界燃烧模拟中热力学性质预测的精度和泛化能力，且该方法仅依赖求解器已有变量，易于集成。

Abstract: Real-fluid thermodynamic property evaluation is a major computational cost in supercritical combustion simulations. In the enthalpy-based pressure-correction formulation, the closure evaluates temperature T, density $ρ$, and compressibility coefficient $ψ$ from the solver state (h,p,Y) through enthalpy-temperature inversion and repeated real-fluid equation-of-state evaluations. Neural-network surrogates offer fixed-cost inference, but direct mapping from (h,p,Y) to $(T,ρ,ψ)$ must capture the enthalpy-temperature relation and non-ideal equation-of-state response, resulting in a complex regression problem. This work introduces a thermodynamics-informed input reparameterization strategy, termed target-aligned input reparameterization (TAIR). TAIR replaces the raw enthalpy coordinate of each property network with a target-matched thermodynamic coordinate: the temperature network uses a temperature estimate obtained by inverting a constant-$c_p$ ideal-gas mixture enthalpy approximation, whereas the density and compressibility networks use an ideal-gas density estimate. These algebraic transformations use only solver-available variables and species constants, guiding the networks to learn real-fluid departures from ideal-gas baselines rather than reconstructing the full closure from raw enthalpy. The method is assessed using supercritical methane-oxygen counterflow flame data against a raw-input baseline and target-inconsistent cross-reparameterization controls. TAIR reduces held-out RMSE by factors of about 1.5, 2.0, and 7.5 for T, $ρ$, and $ψ$, respectively. For an unseen strain-rate flame within the augmented thermodynamic envelope, the corresponding factors are 3.6, 14.5, and 6.0. The target-inconsistent controls perform worse, indicating that the gains arise from thermodynamically matched input design rather than generic preprocessing.

</details>


### [186] [Benchmarking Generalization in Financial Statement Fraud Detection: robust evaluation and novel tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19259)
*Guy Stephane Waffo Dzuyo,Gaël Guibon,Christophe Cerisara,Luis Belmar-Letelier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的财务报表舞弊检测框架，通过整合结构化财务数据与MD&A非结构化文本信息，并构建公司隔离（CI-FSFD）基准任务实现更现实的泛化性能评估。实验表明，该框架在CI-FSFD任务上取得最优性能，验证了文本数据与鲁棒评估对欺诈检测的关键价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财务报表舞弊检测方法存在两大问题：（1）文本数据（如MD&A）利用不足；（2）依赖随机数据划分导致性能评估过于乐观，无法反映模型对新公司或未来时期的真实泛化能力。因此需要更鲁棒的评估框架与多模态信息融合方法。

Method: 提出基于LLM的FSFD框架，同时处理结构化财务数据与非结构化文本（MD&A摘要）；设计公司隔离的CI-FSFD基准任务，确保训练和测试集中的公司不重叠，从而评估真实泛化能力；构建并公开包含美国公司财务报表、MD&A文本摘要及欺诈标签的综合数据集。

Result: 在更具挑战性的CI-FSFD任务上，所提方法取得最优性能，验证了文本数据的价值以及鲁棒评估对于可靠财务欺诈检测的重要性。

Conclusion: 文本数据（如MD&A）与结构化财务数据的融合能显著提升财务报表舞弊检测的泛化性能；公司隔离的评估范式（CI-FSFD）比传统随机划分更贴近实际应用场景；LLM在多模态财务欺诈检测中具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Financial statement fraud detection (FSFD) is crucial for market integrity but faces challenges from increasingly sophisticated schemes and under-utilized textual data in financial reports. Existing methods often rely on random data splits, leading to overoptimistic performance estimates that do not reflect real-world generalization to new companies or future periods. To address this recurring problem with the state of the art, we propose a robust FSFD framework leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to integrate both structured financial data and unstructured textual information from financial reports. We provide a more realistic evaluation through a novel and challenging benchmark task called Company-Isolated FSFD (CI-FSFD). We construct and make publicly available a comprehensive U.S. company dataset combining financial statements, summarized MD&A text, and fraud labels. Our approach achieves the best performance on the challenging CI-FSFD task, demonstrating the critical value of textual data and robust evaluation for reliable financial fraud detection.

</details>


### [187] [Toward Auditable Fraud Detection: Combining Graph Features, Model Explanations, and Agentic Case Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19266)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于PaySim数据集的层级欺诈检测系统，发现图特征和异常信号仅在中等置信度子集上提升性能，对全测试集无改进；图特征能完全恢复注入的多账户欺诈环；LLM调查代理在决策准确性上不如直接阈值分类器，其"合理"解释往往掩盖了错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测系统需要随交易量扩展，同时保持可解释性和可审查性，因此研究层级流水线的各组件在什么条件下真正有效。

Method: 构建层级检测流水线，包括梯度提升分类器、图结构特征、自编码器异常信号、TreeSHAP解释和基于LLM的调查代理。先识别并移除数据集中模拟器特定的余额捷径。通过注入多账户欺诈环进行对照实验评估各组件贡献。

Result: 修正捷径后，图特征和异常信号在全测试集上未提升平均精度，但在中间分数子集上改善了欺诈排名；图特征完全恢复注入的欺诈环，而基线漏检约四分之一；LLM代理准确率65.0%，低于直接阈值的71.7%，且其更改的8个决策中有6个将正确输出改为错误。

Conclusion: 层级欺诈系统的每个组件仅在特定条件下发挥作用；来自调查代理的合理理由不能证明更好的决策质量；基于分歧的升级规则可标记代理错误供人工审查。

Abstract: Fraud detection systems must scale with rising transaction volume while remaining explainable and reviewable. We study a layered pipeline on the PaySim dataset that combines a gradient-boosted classifier, graph-derived structural features, an autoencoder-based anomaly signal, TreeSHAP explanations, and a bounded LLM investigation agent applied to cases the classifier scores uncertainly. Before any model comparison, we identify and remove a simulator-specific balance shortcut that would otherwise inflate baseline performance. After this correction, neither the graph features nor the anomaly signal improves Average Precision on the full test set. Both, however, rank fraud better within the subset of cases receiving intermediate baseline scores. In a controlled experiment with injected multi-account fraud rings, engineered structural features recover all injected test transactions, while the tabular baseline misses roughly a quarter of them. The investigation agent underperforms direct thresholding of the classifier it relies on, reaching 65.0% accuracy against 71.7% on a balanced 60-case sample, despite having access to model explanations, graph context, and retrieved reference cases. Of the eight decisions the agent changed, six replaced correct classifier outputs with errors, and it produced a coherent written rationale in each case. An exploratory disagreement-based escalation rule flagged two of these agent errors for human review without flagging any correct decision. We conclude that each component of a layered fraud system contributes only under specific conditions, and that a plausible rationale from an investigation agent is not evidence of a better decision.

</details>


### [188] [GUIDED Network-Agnostic Feature Initialization for Spatial Transferability in GNN-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19270)
*Alessandro Scalese,Santhanakrishnan Narayanan,Constantinos Antoniou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对交通分配问题中GNN模型的空间泛化瓶颈，提出了一种名为GUIDED的网络无关初始化层。该层通过将出行需求作为标量属性注入虚拟链路而非节点特征，统一了不同网络拓扑的输入空间。与HetGAT模型结合后，该方法在单网络任务上保持最优精度，对分布外需求模式具有更强鲁棒性，数据稀缺时表现优异，并支持低参数量的跨网络迁移学习，同时训练时间减少约50%。该框架还可推广至其他起讫点空间问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GNN代理模型依赖传导性特征初始化，将出行需求与固定网络拓扑绑定，导致模型无法无缝迁移至新的城市环境，存在严重的空间泛化差距，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出GUIDED（几何无约束归纳需求嵌入）初始化层，将出行需求作为标量属性注入辅助虚拟链路，而非传统节点特征，从而标准化不同规模网络下的输入空间，实现网络无关的特征初始化。集成到HetGAT（异构图注意力网络）模型中。

Result: 在多个城市拓扑上的实验表明：(1) 单网络任务保持最先进预测精度；(2) 对分布外需求模式具有更强鲁棒性；(3) 在严重数据稀缺下仍保持明显优势；(4) 支持高参数效率的跨网络迁移学习；(5) 优化后的散射操作使每epoch训练时间减少约50%。

Conclusion: GUIDED层为真正归纳式（inductive）的交通分配模型建立了稳健基础，其对空间拓扑的抽象为货运物流、多模式网络优化等广义起讫点空间问题提供了通用蓝图。

Abstract: The Traffic Assignment Problem is a fundamental but computationally expensive component of transportation planning. While Graph Neural Networks have emerged as fast, data-driven surrogates, their practical deployment is severely constrained by a spatial generalization gap. Standard models rely on transductive feature initializations that tie travel demand to fixed network topologies, preventing seamless transfer to new urban environments. To overcome this structural limitation, this research proposes a network-agnostic initialization layer, termed Geometrically Unconstrained Inductive Demand EmbeDding (GUIDED). By injecting travel demand as a scalar attribute on auxiliary virtual links rather than as specific node features, this modular framework standardizes the input space regardless of network scale. Extensive experimental evaluation across multiple urban topologies demonstrates that a Heterogeneous Graph Attention Network (HetGAT) model integrated with the proposed GUIDED layer maintains state-of-the-art predictive accuracy on single-network tasks, while demonstrating superior robustness to out-of-distribution demand patterns and maintaining a distinct performance advantage over the baseline even under severe data scarcity. Notably, the proposed feature initialization enables highly parameter-efficient domain adaptation for inter-network transfer learning without artificial input homogenization, establishing a robust foundation for truly inductive models. At the same time, the optimized scatter operations of the initialization layer yield an approximate 50% reduction in training time per epoch compared to the baseline approach. Furthermore, while demonstrated on vehicular traffic, this fundamental abstraction of spatial topology provides a versatile blueprint for generalized origin-destination spatial problems, such as freight logistics and multimodal network optimization.

</details>


### [189] [A Reinforcement-Learning-Augmented Liquid-Fueled Reactor Network Model for Predicting Lean Blowout in Gas Turbine Combustors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19281)
*Philip John,Eloghosa Ikponmwoba,Pinaki Pal,Opeoluwa Owoyele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种强化学习框架，用于生成最优液体燃料反应器，以改善燃气轮机燃烧室中的贫油熄火（LBO）预测。该方法采用多阶段聚类-分类策略：先用k-means生成大量均匀微簇，再通过actor-critic RL智能体将其合并为最优反应区域。使用Jet-A机理（119种组分，841个反应）的验证表明，该框架相比k-means提高了预测精度，捕捉了正确的LBO趋势，同时相比高保真计算模型实现了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定聚类边界的方法依赖手动启发式或输入空间中的距离度量，未显式考虑目标指标（如LBO预测精度），导致生成的聚类可能不利于目标任务的预测性能。

Method: 多阶段聚类-分类策略：第一阶段使用k-means聚类生成大量均匀微簇；第二阶段采用actor-critic强化学习智能体，以LBO预测精度为优化目标，将微簇逐步合并为最优反应区域。

Result: 在Jet-A机理（119种组分，841个反应）上的验证表明，RL框架相比k-means具有更高的预测保真度，能够捕捉正确的LBO趋势，同时相比高保真计算模型实现了显著的计算加速。

Conclusion: 该RL驱动的反应器网络生成方法作为一种计算高效的降阶建模技术，具有强大的潜力，可补充高保真仿真，用于快速设计空间探索。

Abstract: This study introduces a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for generating optimal liquid-fueled reactors to improve lean blowout (LBO) predictions in gas turbine combustors. Existing approaches for determining cluster boundaries rely on manual heuristics or distance-based metrics in the input space. In contrast, the proposed method is goal-oriented, explicitly accounting for the target metric (e.g., LBO prediction accuracy) during cluster formation. The framework employs a multi-stage clustering--classification strategy: an initial clustering step (e.g., $k$-means clustering) generates a large set of homogeneous micro-clusters, followed by an actor-critic RL agent that merges them into optimal reactor zones. The validation study, performed using a Jet-A mechanism (119 species, 841 reactions), shows the RL framework offers improved predictive fidelity compared to $k$-means and captures the correct LBO trends, while achieving substantial speedups relative to the high-fidelity computational model. Overall, the RL-driven approach demonstrates strong potential as a computationally efficient reduced-order modeling technique that can complement high-fidelity simulations for rapid design-space exploration.

</details>


### [190] [Riemannian Deep Learning:Modules, Networks, and Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19305)
*Chen Ziheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的黎曼深度学习框架，从可复用神经模块、流形特定网络架构和底层几何设计三个视角出发，将批归一化推广到李群和陀螺群，将多项逻辑回归推广到SPD流形及一般黎曼流形，开发了双曲空间、Busemann双曲学习和满秩相关矩阵的神经网络，并引入了SPD流形上可学习且计算高效的黎曼度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络在流形值表示上的研究存在三个主要问题：许多基本组件局限于特定流形、依赖欧几里得近似、或需要昂贵且数值不稳定的几何运算。亟需一个统一的黎曼深度学习框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 将批归一化从欧氏空间和单个流形推广到李群和陀螺群；2) 将多项逻辑回归从欧氏空间推广到SPD流形再到一般黎曼流形；3) 开发双曲空间的无约束模型、Busemann双曲学习架构和满秩相关矩阵网络；4) 引入SPD流形上可学习的Log-Euclidean度量和基于Cholesky分解的快速稳定几何。

Result: 通过理论分析和在视觉、信号处理、图学习和基因组学等领域的数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文从三个互补视角构建了统一的黎曼深度学习框架，克服了现有方法在流形适应性、计算效率和数值稳定性方面的局限，为流形值表示的深度学习提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks on manifold-valued representations have attracted growing interest, but many basic components remain tied to specific manifolds, rely on Euclidean approximations, or require costly and numerically fragile geometric operations. This thesis develops a unified framework for Riemannian deep learning from three complementary perspectives: reusable neural modules, manifold-specific network architectures, and the design of underlying geometries. It generalizes batch normalization from Euclidean spaces and individual manifolds to broad classes of Lie groups and gyrogroups, and extends multinomial logistic regression from Euclidean space to SPD manifolds and then to general Riemannian manifolds. It further develops neural networks for several important geometric representations, including an unconstrained model of hyperbolic space, Busemann-based hyperbolic learning, and full-rank correlation matrices. Finally, it introduces adaptive and computationally efficient Riemannian metrics on SPD manifolds, including learnable Log-Euclidean geometries and fast, stable Cholesky-based geometries. The proposed methods are supported by theoretical analysis and validated through numerical experiments and applications in vision, signal processing, graph learning, and genomics.

</details>


### [191] [Staypoint Detection from Noisy Trajectory Data [Experiment Paper]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19312)
*Lance Kennedy,Hossein Amiri,Yueyang Liu,Riyang Bao,Hanqi Chen,Mohammad Hashemi,Ruochen Kong,Xiaotong Liu,Joon-Seok Kim,Shengpu Tang,Liang Zhao,Andreas Züfle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了16个大规模模拟数据集和9种停留点检测算法（包括监督和无监督方法）的基准评估，发现现有算法在噪声条件下表现不佳，而提出的无监督方法有显著改进，监督方法则大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停留点检测作为将原始轨迹数据转化为语义意义位置（如家、工作地、餐厅）的基础步骤，对空间计算应用至关重要，但缺乏标准基准数据集和系统性算法评估。

Method: (1) 构建16个大规模模拟数据集，包含数千个代理及其带注释的停留点，并具有不同的轨迹噪声水平；(2) 评估9种停留点检测算法，涵盖现有最先进方法和新提出的方法，分析其对噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 现有最先进算法在真实噪声条件下表现不佳；提出的无监督方法有显著改进；监督方法大幅超越现有基线。

Conclusion: 该数据集和方法仅作为未来停留点检测研究的起点。

Abstract: Detecting staypoints from raw trajectory data is fundamental to numerous spatial computing applications. This process transforms raw numeric sequences of geolocations into semantically meaningful locations, such as homes, workplaces, or restaurants. Despite its importance for semantic trajectory analysis, staypoint detection lacks standard benchmarks, and existing algorithms have never been systematically evaluated. This gap persists because no publicly available datasets provide both raw individual trajectories and ground-truth staypoint annotations. This benchmark paper addresses this limitation with two key contributions: (1) we introduce 16 large-scale simulated datasets capturing thousands of agents with annotated staypoints across varying trajectory noise levels, and (2) we evaluate nine staypoint detection algorithms-including both state-of-the-art and novel methods-to analyze their robustness to noise. Our evaluation reveals that existing state-of-the-art algorithms perform poorly under realistic noise conditions. Conversely, our proposed unsupervised methods yield substantial improvements, while supervised approaches drastically outperform existing baselines. While these results are very promising, these datasets and methods are only meant as starting points for future research in staypoint detection.

</details>


### [192] [Off-Context GRPO: Learning to Reason on Hard Problems using Privileged Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19313)
*Priyank Agrawal,Ankur Samanta,Shervin Ghasemlou,Jalaj Bhandari,Kavosh Asadi,Daniel Jiang,Aditya Modi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO)，通过在训练中使用带特权引导（如解题前缀）的rollout并应用重要性校正目标，解决了RLVR在难题上因零奖励导致无学习信号的问题，在数学推理基准上平均提升3.9%的绝对性能（13.8%的相对提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型RLVR方法在难题上失效：当模型无法生成任何正确答案时，奖励信号为零，导致无法学习。作者希望通过在训练中提供特权引导（如解题前缀）来帮助模型获得非零奖励信号。

Method: 提出Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO)，是GRPO的最小修改变体。关键思想是使用带特权引导的rollout（称为off-context rollout），但通过应用重要性校正目标将更新方向拉回原始无引导目标，从而避免引导训练中目标不匹配导致的不稳定问题。

Result: 在标准数学推理基准上，OC-GRPO相比原始GRPO平均实现3.9%的绝对提升（13.8%的相对提升），且额外计算成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: OC-GRPO有效地解决了RLVR在难题上的零学习信号问题，通过带引导的rollout和重要性校正机制，在不显著增加成本的情况下显著提升了数学推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning in large language models. Yet, typical RLVR approaches fail on difficult problems: when a model cannot generate any correct solutions, it receives \textit{zero} learning signal. Providing privileged guidance during training, such as solution prefixes, can help overcome this learning cliff by steering the model towards {correct solutions with non-zero reward}. {We call these rollouts \textit{off-context}: they are generated from a training prompt that contains privileged guidance, while the target objective is defined by the original prompt without that guidance.} {We introduce} Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO), a minimally modified variant of GRPO that uses guided rollouts but applies an importance-corrected objective to steer the update back toward the original unguided objective, avoiding the mismatch that destabilizes uncorrected guided training. Empirically, our algorithm achieves a 3.9\% absolute improvement (13.8\% relative gain) over vanilla GRPO on average across standard mathematical reasoning benchmarks with negligible additional cost.

</details>


### [193] [CircuitKIT : Circuit Discovery, Evaluation, and Application Toolkit for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19317)
*Pratinav Seth,Hem Gosalia,Aditya Kasliwal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CircuitKIT是一个开源库，通过类型化、可序列化的表示连接电路分析的完整工作流程，包含发现算法、声明式接口、诊断工具和应用模块，旨在标准化和简化电路分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电路分析方法碎片化严重，不同环节（发现、评估、干预）需要拼接独立实现，且许多方法需要手动编写对比提示，导致方法难以比较且应用受限。

Method: 提出CircuitKIT库，提供类型化可序列化表示统一电路分析工作流，包含：发现算法套件、结构化数据到发现任务的声明式接口、电路诊断工具以及下游应用模块。

Result: 发布了完整的库、示例代码、使用笔记本和文档（https://github.com/Lexsi-Labs/CircuitKIT），为电路分析的开展和比较提供了通用基础设施。

Conclusion: CircuitKIT通过统一的类型化表示和模块化组件，有效解决了电路分析中方法碎片化的问题，促进了不同方法的比较和更广泛的应用。

Abstract: Circuit analysis can support not only model explanation but also downstream interventions such as pruning, editing, steering, and selective fine-tuning. However, conducting such analyses currently requires stitching together separate implementations for discovery, evaluation, and intervention, as well as hand-authoring the contrastive prompts required by many discovery methods. This fragmentation makes methods difficult to compare and limits their application beyond canonical tasks. We introduce CircuitKIT, a source-available library that connects the circuit-analysis workflow through a typed, serializable representation. CircuitKIT provides a suite of discovery algorithms, declarative interfaces for mapping structured data into discovery tasks, complementary circuit diagnostics, and downstream application modules. Together, these components provide common infrastructure for conducting and comparing circuit analyses. The library, examples, notebooks, and documentation are released at https://github.com/Lexsi-Labs/CircuitKIT .

</details>


### [194] [ISO: An RLVR-Native Optimization Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19331)
*Hanqing Zhu,Wenyan Cong,Zhizhou Sha,Sagnik Mukherjee,Xinyuan Song,David González-Martínez,Xiaoxia Wu,Yuandong Tian,Shiwei Liu,David Z. Pan,Zhangyang "Atlas" Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等谱优化(ISO)框架，揭示了RLVR中的谱继承现象：强化学习可重用基模型的权重谱，仅通过改变输入/输出奇异帧来获取新行为。ISO包含离线(ISO-Merger)和在线(ISO-Optimizer)两种实例，在推理和编码任务上相比基线方法显著提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽在提升语言模型推理能力方面进展迅速，但将奖励反馈转化为权重空间更新的优化层仍未得到充分理解，缺乏专门针对后训练阶段设计的优化框架。

Method: 通过分析模型权重的奇异结构发现谱继承现象，据此提出等谱优化(ISO)框架：离线ISO-Merger将共享基座专家的帧变化合并为单一等谱模型；在线ISO-Optimizer在固定基座谱的同时优化帧变量，兼容AdamW和Muon等基础优化器。

Result: ISO-Merger在无数据合并方法中取得最强聚合性能；ISO-Optimizer在1.5B至8B参数的推理和编码任务中显著减少训练步数，如Qwen3-8B-Base上ISO-AdamW仅用100步即达到AdamW需270步才能达到的准确率。

Conclusion: 后训练优化不应全盘继承预训练优化方式，而应围绕奖励驱动适应的结构进行设计：继承谱，优化帧。ISO为RLVR缺失的优化层提供了具体答案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is rapidly advancing the reasoning capabilities of language models, yet the optimization layer that converts reward feedback into weight-space updates remains poorly understood. Building on our prior analysis (Zhu et al., 2025), we study this missing layer through the singular structure of model weights and identify spectral inheritance: RLVR can reuse the base model's weight spectra while acquiring new behavior through changes in the associated input and output singular frames.
  We operationalize spectral inheritance as Isospectral Optimization (ISO), an RLVR-native, fixed-spectrum optimization framework with complementary offline and online instantiations. Offline, ISO-Merger combines the frame changes of shared-base specialists into a single fixed-spectrum model, requiring no post-merge data, rollouts, gradient updates, or on-policy distillation (OPD). It recovers complementary specialist capabilities and achieves the strongest aggregate performance among the compared data-free merging methods. Online, ISO-Optimizer applies a chosen base optimizer, including AdamW and Muon, to the frame variables while keeping the base spectra fixed. Across reasoning and coding tasks ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters, ISO-Optimizer improves accuracy in the reported runs and reaches matched scores with substantially fewer training steps. On Qwen3-8B-Base, AdamW reaches an aggregate accuracy of 0.495 after 270 training steps. ISO-AdamW reaches the same accuracy after only 100 training steps and improves further to 0.509 after 210 training steps. Together, ISO offers a concrete answer to RLVR's missing optimization layer: rather than inheriting pre-training optimization wholesale, design post-training around the structure of reward-driven adaptation: inherit the spectrum, optimize the frames.

</details>


### [195] [ROMS-IMLE: A Minimalist Approach to Competitive Single-Step Generative Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19332)
*Chirag Vashist,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了扩散模型"需要通过多个小步骤逐步将噪声分布转换为数据分布"的常见信念，并采用极简主义方法设计了一个单步生成模型。该模型使用隐式最大似然估计（IMLE）作为训练目标，搭配中等规模的卷积网络，避免使用迭代去噪、变分推理、对抗训练或Transformer。在ImageNet 256上取得了2.56的FID，同时具备良好的精度和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑当前生成模型（特别是扩散模型和流匹配）中"需要通过多次小变换逐步将噪声分布转换为数据分布"这一普遍信念的必要性，探究是否可以用更简单的方法设计出具有竞争力的生成模型。

Method: 采用极简主义方法，选择IMLE作为训练目标（避免变分推理、对抗训练和数值积分等复杂方案），使用中等规模的卷积网络而非Transformer，并有意避免迭代去噪过程，仅添加真正必要的元素。

Result: 在ImageNet 256上实现了2.56的FID，同时保持了良好的精度和召回率，是一个单步、参数高效的生成模型。

Conclusion: 迭代去噪并非生成模型取得优异性能的必要条件，通过精心选择的训练目标和简化的模型架构，可以在单步生成中达到具有竞争力的效果，挑战了当前扩散模型的主导范式。

Abstract: Generative models have undergone many generations of evolution, from VAEs/GANs to diffusion/flow matching. Along the way, the underlying techniques have become more complicated and various beliefs about what drives strong empirical performance have taken hold. Due to the success of diffusion models and flow matching, one of the more common beliefs is the importance of transforming the noise distribution to the data distribution gradually through many small transformations. We ask whether this is truly necessary, and take a minimalist approach to designing a competitive generative model. We start with the bare-bones essentials, namely just a training objective and a model. We purposefully make both simple. For the training objective, we choose Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE), and eschew more complicated alternatives such as variational inference, adversarial training and numerical integration. For the model, we eschew transformers and instead choose a moderately sized convolutional network. Then we judiciously added elements that are truly essential, which surprisingly do not include iterative denoising. The result is a single-step parameter-efficient generative model that produces high quality samples at fast speed: it achieves an FID of 2.56 on ImageNet 256 and simultaneously attains good precision and recall.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [$\texttt{codesign-mcdp}$: A Python Library for Monotone Co-Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18415)
*Corentin Briat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: codesign-mcdp是一个用于在Censi(2015)框架下求解单调协同设计问题(MCDPs)的Python库，实现了反链演算、六种基本设计问题类型、三种组合算子、Kleene不动点求解器，以及多种高级功能模块和示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化地解决单调协同设计问题，提供可组合、可扩展的计算框架，支持从功能需求到最小资源消耗的反链求解。

Method: 基于反链演算，实现了六种基本设计问题类型、串行/并行/反馈三种组合算子、Kleene不动点求解器；提供MCDPL风格声明式构建器和模块化System构建器；扩展层支持集合和随机不确定性、组合在线学习、时间/向量状态/在线协同设计。

Result: 开发了一个完整的Python库，支持MCDP的公式化、求解和组合，附带多组工作示例。

Conclusion: codesign-mcdp为单调协同设计问题的理论研究和工程实践提供了一个系统化、可扩展的计算工具。

Abstract: $\texttt{codesign-mcdp}$ is a Python library for formulating and solving $\textit{Monotone Co-Design Problems}$ (MCDPs) in the framework of Censi (2015). A design problem is a relation between two posets, a functionality poset $F$ and a resource poset $R$; given a target functionality, the problem asks for the antichain of minimal resources needed to deliver it. Design problems compose under three operators (series, parallel, feedback), and the resulting class is closed under composition. The library implements the antichain calculus, six primitive design-problem types, the three composition operators, a Kleene fixed-point solver, and two high-level builders (an MCDPL-style declarative builder and a modular $\texttt{System}$ builder). Further layers add set-based and stochastic uncertainty, compositional online learning, and temporal, vector-state, and online co-design, alongside a suite of worked examples.

</details>


### [197] [A National-Scale EV Charging Scheduling Framework: Optimal Detour Routing Under Infrastructure Capacity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18453)
*Taner Cokyasar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的优化框架，用于在现有充电基础设施下调度电动汽车的长途旅行充电计划，并在美国约270万条真实轨迹和14,260个充电站的数据上验证，能在1.3小时内生成可行调度。同时进行三层次经济分析，评估电动汽车相比燃油车的成本竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电动汽车普及，量化长途旅行中现有充电基础设施带来的调度负担和经济成本，对基础设施规划和政策制定至关重要。

Method: 提出基于优化的三阶段框架：(i) 通过前向可达性启发式剪枝不可行路径；(ii) 在有向无环图上用动态规划计算每辆车的最优充电计划；(iii) 通过基于惩罚的启发式方法迭代解决容量约束，对拥堵站点增加绕路成本，并辅以先入先出队列回退策略。

Result: 在约270万条轨迹、14,260个DC快充站（68,641个充电插头）的数据上，框架在128核HPC集群上不到1.3小时即可生成容量可行的充电调度，无需商业优化求解器。

Conclusion: 该框架可有效评估大规模电动汽车长途旅行的充电调度可行性及经济性，为充电基础设施规划和电动汽车成本竞争力分析提供支持。

Abstract: As electric vehicle (EV) adoption grows, quantifying the scheduling burden and economic cost of long-distance travel under the existing charging infrastructure becomes increasingly important for infrastructure planning and policy. This paper presents a scalable, optimization-based framework for scheduling EV charging stops along real-world charging stations and simulated long-distance personal vehicle trajectories across the United States using POLARIS. Taking the existing charging network as fixed input, the framework minimizes total detour and queuing costs for each vehicle while respecting plug capacity constraints at each station. The methodology proceeds in three phases: (i) infeasibility pruning via a forward-pass reachability heuristic, (ii) per-vehicle optimal charging schedule computation via dynamic programming on a directed acyclic graph, and (iii) capacity-aware iterative congestion resolution through a penalty-based heuristic that augments detour costs at congested stations, with a first-in, first-out queue fallback. Applied to approximately 2.7M origin--destination vehicle trajectories derived from a 1\% sample of national personal travel demand within the POLARIS agent-based transportation simulation framework and covering 14,260 DC fast charging stations with 68,641 plugs from the Alternative Fuels Station Locator, the framework produces capacity-feasible schedules in under 1.3 hours on a 128-core high-performance computing cluster without requiring any commercial optimization solver. A three-tier economic analysis spanning operational costs, total cost of ownership, and amortized infrastructure investment is conducted to evaluate EV cost competitiveness relative to internal combustion engine vehicles across scenarios.

</details>


### [198] [Revisit escape path for infinite unit strip forest and unit broadworm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18563)
*Zhipeng Deng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Zalgaller无限单位带状森林和单位尺蠖逃逸路径问题的新解析公式和新方法，将问题重构为区间覆盖问题，进而转化为带约束的泛函最小化问题，并通过直接离散化和凸优化求解。最后证明封闭逃逸路径的最优解是单位宽度常值曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Zalgaller的逃逸路径问题仅通过几何方法研究，缺乏解析公式和计算框架。作者希望提供更通用的数值求解方法，并给出解析解。

Method: 将问题重新表述为区间覆盖问题，再转化为带约束的泛函最小化问题；通过直接离散化将连续优化转化为凸优化问题求解。

Result: 得到了Zalgaller问题的解析公式；证明了对于单位带状森林的封闭逃逸路径，最优解是单位宽度常值曲线。

Conclusion: 所提出的区间覆盖和凸优化框架是解决此类逃逸路径问题的有效方法，并能推广到其他线段分析。

Abstract: Building on our previous general computational solution to Bellman's Lost-in-a-Forest Problem, we present a new approach and analytical formulas for the previously well-known escape path for the infinite unit-strip forest and unit broadworm by Zalgaller. Earlier studies addressed these problems exclusively through geometric methods. We reformulated the problem as an interval-cover problem and then formulated it as a constrained functional minimization problem. This constrained functional minimization can be directly discretized and subsequently solved as a convex optimization. Furthermore, we extend the analysis of various line segment. Finally, we show that, in the case of a closed escape path for the unit strip, the optimal solution is a curve of constant unit width.

</details>


### [199] [Fragility of Minimum-Variance Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18624)
*Daniel Ovalle,Carl D. Laird,Ignacio E. Grossmann,Javier Peña*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过块对角相关结构推导出仅做多最小方差投资组合的闭式解，揭示了估计脆弱性源于波动率与相关结构的阈值效应，并提出了基于结构化收缩的稳健化方法，在异质波动率场景下通过诱导稀疏性来规避高风险资产，实证表明该方法在样本外方差和换手率上优于传统基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小方差投资组合对协方差估计误差高度敏感，现有全局收缩方法缺乏对脆弱性来源的深入分析，需要一种能明确揭示并针对性处理该敏感性的理论框架和实用方法。

Method: 对协方差矩阵施加块对角相关结构约束，推导出仅做多最小方差投资组合权重的闭式解；基于阈值效应分析提出相关性感知的结构化收缩方案，并通过聚类近似扩展到一般协方差矩阵。

Result: 在波动率同质环境下，忽略相关的收缩方法在风险与稳定性之间取得最佳平衡；在异质波动率环境下，相关性感知的收缩通过诱导稀疏性并规避高风险资产表现最优。所提方法在样本外方差和换手率上均优于经典和基于聚类的基准方法。

Conclusion: 论文提供了理论上可解释、实践上可行的稳健投资组合构建框架，通过区分不同波动率环境选择合适的收缩策略，能够有效降低样本外风险并提升组合稳定性。

Abstract: Minimum-variance portfolios are well known to be highly sensitive to covariance estimation error. In this paper, we show that by imposing a block diagonal correlation structure, we can derive closed-form expressions for long-only minimum-variance portfolios that make this fragility explicit. These analytical solutions reveal that fragility is driven by threshold effects arising from the interaction between correlation structure and the assets' volatilities. Motivated by the latter insight, we propose robustification approaches that can be interpreted as structured shrinkage schemes that selectively attenuate unstable coupling while preserving the dominant risk structure. Unlike global shrinkage techniques, the proposed corrections are analytically grounded, require minimal tuning, and remain closely aligned with the minimum-variance solution. The framework extends naturally to general covariance matrices through clustering-based approximations. Empirical results on controlled simulations highlight a clear regime dependence. In homogeneous volatility settings, correlation-oblivious shrinkage achieves the best trade-off between risk and stability. In contrast, under heterogeneous volatility, correlation-aware shrinkage performs best by inducing sparsity and avoiding exposure to high-risk assets. Across regimes, the proposed methods consistently reduce out-of-sample variance and turnover relative to classical and clustering-based benchmarks, providing a principled and practical approach to robust portfolio construction.

</details>


### [200] [Directional Derivatives and Error Bounds of Merit Functions in Vector Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18781)
*Yu Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了向量优化问题中效益函数θ的次微分、方向导数与误差界，建立了方向导数与解集几何之间的完整桥梁，为算法收敛性分析提供了基础工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为向量优化问题的效益函数θ提供完整的次微分、方向导数及误差界分析，填补该领域理论空白，为算法收敛性分析奠定基础。

Method: 利用弱*紧凸集K=$\overline{\operatorname{co}}^{w^*}(S(C^+))$推导θ的对偶表示；在弱有效解处显式表达方向导数；建立13种全局误差界条件的等价性；通过单位球面上最小方向导数的均匀负性条件刻画全局误差界性质。

Result: 证明了θ是全局凹且Lipschitz连续的；得到了方向导数的显式公式$θ'(\bar x;d)=\min_{y^*\in W(\bar x)}\langle y^*,F(d)\rangle$；建立了13种全局误差界条件的等价性；确定了最优局部误差界常数为$1/\varphi(\bar x)$；给出了$\varphi(\bar x)=0$时需要额外方向条件的反例。

Conclusion: 本文完整建立了向量优化中效益函数的次微分、方向导数与解集几何之间的理论联系，其全局/局部误差界刻画为向量优化算法的收敛性分析提供了关键理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of directional derivatives and error bounds for the merit function $θ(x)=\sup_{a\in A}\bigl(-Δ_C(F(x)-F(a))\bigr)$ associated with the vector optimization problem $\operatorname{Min}_C\{F(x):x\in A\}$, where $Δ_C$ is the oriented distance function. We first prove that $θ$ is concave and Lipschitz continuous on the whole space and derive its dual representation via the weak$^*$ compact convex set $K=\overline{\operatorname{co}}^{w^*}(S(C^+))$. At a weakly efficient solution $\bar x$, we obtain the explicit formula $θ'(\bar x;d)=\min_{y^*\in W(\bar x)}\langle y^*,F(d)\rangle$ with $W(\bar{x})=\{y^*\in K:F^*y^*\in -N_A(\bar{x})\}$, characterize the zero-directional-derivative cone, and prove that, under a local error bound condition, the tangent cone to the solution set is $T_{E_w}(\bar x)=T_A(\bar x)\cap T_{\widehat A}(\bar x)=\{d\in T_A(\bar x):θ'(\bar x;d)=0\}$. We establish the equivalence of thirteen distinct global error bound conditions, including characterizations via linear regularity, the global slope, an asymptotic condition, and perturbation stability. A central result shows that the global error bound property for $θ$ on the feasible set $A$ is characterized by a uniform negativity condition on the unit-sphere minimal directional derivative, namely $\sup_{x \in A \setminus E_w} \varphi(x) < 0$. We also determine the optimal local error bound constant precisely as $1/\varphi(\bar x)$ when $\varphi(\bar x)>0$, and provide a counterexample demonstrating that an additional directional condition is essential when $\varphi(\bar x)=0$. These results provide a complete bridge between the directional derivative of the merit function and the geometry of the solution set, offering fundamental tools for the convergence analysis of algorithms in vector optimization.

</details>


### [201] [A frugal primal-dual splitting with minimal lifting over arbitrary rooted trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18932)
*Feng Xue,Hui Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有最小提升的节俭原始-对偶分裂方法，用于求解涉及共余算子、线性组合和平行和的单调包含问题。该方法基于树形图建立层次节点和边，允许任意分配对偶变量和共余元素到原始节点，实现了分布式计算的灵活性。该方法能自然扩展Douglas-Rachford分裂、恢复并行Chambolle-Pock算法，并解决一类结构化凸最小化问题，具有O(1/k)的原始-对偶间隙遍历率，通过乘积希尔伯特空间中的重构技术推导出渐近正则性的o(1/k)率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理包含共余算子、线性组合和平行和的复杂结构化单调包含问题时，通常存在提升维度高、计算效率低或缺乏灵活性等问题。本文旨在设计一种"节俭的"（frugal）方法，即用最小的变量提升（lifting）来求解这类问题，同时提供灵活的分布式计算能力（集中式或去中心化）。

Method: 在树形结构图上定义层次节点和边，允许对偶变量和共余元素任意分配到原始节点；在乘积希尔伯特空间中引入新的重构技术以简化收敛性分析；推导出渐近正则性的o(1/k)率。

Result: 该方法能够自然扩展Douglas-Rachford分裂到各种图结构，恢复并推广并行Chambolle-Pock算法；在求解一类结构化凸最小化问题时，原始-对偶间隙函数达到O(1/k)的遍历收敛率；通过乘积空间重构技术，进一步得到渐近正则性的o(1/k)率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的节俭原始-对偶分裂框架以最小提升为代价，统一并推广了多种经典分裂算法，兼顾了理论收敛保证和实际分布式计算的灵活性，为求解大规模结构化单调包含问题提供了高效且通用的工具。

Abstract: We develop a frugal primal-dual splitting with minimal lifting for solving structured monotone inclusions, involving cocoercive operators, linear compositions and parallel sums. This is established by defining hierarchical nodes and edges between them over a tree-structured graph, with arbitrary assignments of dual variables and cocoercive elements to primal nodes. This arbitrariness allows a great flexibility in terms of level-synchronous distributed computing, such as centralized or decentralized. The particular instances naturally extend the Douglas--Rachford splitting on various graphs, recover the parallel Chambolle--Pock, and solve a class of structured convex minimization problems with O(1/k) ergodic rate for its primal-dual gap function. Furthermore, we introduce a reformulation technique in a product Hilbert space to facilitate the convergence analysis, specifically to derive the o(1/k)-rate of asymptotic regularity.

</details>


### [202] [Evaluating DV/CV-QKD Architectures for SAFE Long-Term Secure Storage: A Risk Model and ILP-Based Cost Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19010)
*Alireza Tasdighi,Romain Alléaume*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一成本建模与优化框架，用于评估混合DV/CV-QKD基础设施在SAFE长期安全存储协议中的表现，通过耦合定量风险模型与随机整数线性规划，确定了传输预算和安全约束下的成本最优拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QKD网络部署缺乏同时考虑信息论安全与计算耐久性的多年代跨度的统一成本优化方法，需要一种能够联合评估混合DV/CV模态、兼顾实际工业实现与网络拓扑经济性的框架。

Method: 采用定量风险模型与随机整数线性规划（ILP）相结合的方法，配合样本平均近似（SAA）管道，在传输预算和安全约束下求解成本最优的SAFE拓扑结构。

Result: 最优经济架构高度依赖场景；混合与同质模态分配均可自然涌现；发现了最低所需QKD链路容量与基础设施成本之间的非单调关系——提高链路密钥率可能通过转向更经济的CV方案降低总成本，但进一步增加不一定带来额外节省。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效指导下一代QKD网络的经济部署决策，揭示了网络拓扑、QKD模态与部署成本之间的复杂交互关系，为混合DV/CV-QKD基础设施优化提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified cost-modeling and optimization framework designed to evaluate hybrid discrete-variable/continuous-variable quantum key distribution (DV/CV-QKD) infrastructures operating within the SAFE (Secure and Efficient) long-term storage (LTSS) protocol. Our methodology jointly addresses the information-theoretic and computational dimensions of cryptographic durability over multi-decade horizons by coupling a quantitative risk model with a stochastic integer linear programming (ILP) formulation. Going beyond idealized physics-informed limits, the framework incorporates realistic next-generation industrial implementations and multiplexed coexistence specifications, together with a sample-average approximation (SAA) pipeline, to determine cost-optimal and structurally feasible SAFE topologies under transmission-budget and security constraints. The proposed framework is evaluated on both randomized synthetic deployments and realistic metropolitan-scale QKD infrastructures, including the Paris Metro-Scale and Greater Paris networks, to characterize the interplay between network topology, QKD modality, and deployment cost while enforcing a target global compromise tolerance $ε$. Numerical results show that the economically optimal architecture is highly scenario-dependent and that both hybrid and homogeneous modality allocations naturally emerge from the optimization. More importantly, the analysis reveals a non-monotonic relationship between the minimum required QKD link capacity and the infrastructure cost: contrary to intuition, increasing the minimum required per-link secret-key rate may reduce the overall deployment cost by enabling a global transition toward more economical CV-based solutions, whereas further capacity increases do not necessarily yield additional savings.

</details>


### [203] [Graphical stability of set-valued integrals under measure perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19035)
*Tam Le*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了非光滑优化中参数化集值积分在概率测度弱扰动下的稳定性，证明了在紧参数集、紧凸值且联合外半连续的被积函数条件下，集值积分映射沿弱收敛测度序列在超出距离下图形收敛，并讨论了假设的紧致性及在随机广义方程解集稳定性中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑优化中的采样和近似方案需要理解集值期望在概率分布弱扰动下的稳定性行为。

Method: 通过建立紧凸值且联合外半连续的被积函数在超线性可积条件下的图形收敛理论，分析弱收敛测度序列下集值积分映射的稳定性。

Result: 证明了沿任何弱收敛概率测度序列，集值积分映射在超出距离下图形收敛；联合外半连续性和超线性包络条件具有紧致性。

Conclusion: 该稳定性原理为集值期望的测度近似提供了统一框架，并导出随机广义方程解集的外稳定性。

Abstract: Motivated by sampling and approximation schemes arising in nonsmooth optimization, we study the stability of parameterized set-valued integrals under weak perturbations of the underlying probability distribution. For a compact parameter set, we show that compact convex-valued and jointly outer semicontinuous integrands induce set-valued integral maps that converge graphically in excess distance along any weakly convergent sequence of probability measures. The result holds under a superlinear integrability condition and provides a unified stability principle for measure approximations of set-valued expectations. We discuss the sharpness of the assumptions through examples. In particular, we emphasize that joint outer semicontinuity is required in general and that the superlinear envelope condition is tight relative to the classical i.i.d. empirical setting. As a consequence, we obtain outer stability of solution sets for stochastic generalized equations. We illustrate the stability result in several settings, including stochastic nonsmooth optimization with Markovian sampling, smoothing by mollifiers, and parameter-dependent distributional dynamics.

</details>


### [204] [Industrial Application of a Multi-Disciplinary Design Optimization with Uncertainties to a Pair of Telecommunication Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19045)
*Olivier Sapin,Loïc Cousin,Nicolas Roussouly,Anne Gazaix,François Gallard,Matthias De Lozzo,Xavier Fosse,Nicolas Sarda,Gaspard Berthelin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在卫星设计中采用不确定性下的多学科设计优化方法，替代传统安全裕度做法。以一对堆叠发射的通信卫星为案例，通过敏感性分析和基于可靠性的设计优化，在保证约束高概率可行的前提下，将性能损失降低66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卫星设计通过添加安全裕度来应对不确定性，但这往往牺牲性能。本文旨在通过在设计问题中显式纳入不确定性，在保证鲁棒性的同时减少性能损失。

Method: 应用不确定性下的多学科设计优化（MDO under uncertainty），结合不确定性量化技术（敏感性分析、基于可靠性的设计优化），以一对堆叠发射的通信卫星为工业用例，两卫星各自为强耦合多学科系统但彼此解耦。

Result: 相比预设安全裕度的方法，所提方法将性能损失降低66%，同时以高概率保证约束可行性。

Conclusion: 在卫星设计中，将不确定性纳入设计优化问题比单纯添加安全裕度更有效，能在保证可靠性的同时显著提升性能。

Abstract: In satellite design, it is common practice to add safety margins to the constraints to achieve conservative solutions that are robust to uncertainties. This robustness often comes at the expense of performance and it may be more appropriate to include uncertainties in the definition of the design problem. This work addresses such a challenge by applying techniques of multidisciplinary design optimization under uncertainty to an industrial use case. The latter is a pair of telecommunication satellites launched together in a stacked configuration. Each satellite is a strongly coupled multidisciplinary system while the two satellites are not coupled at all. This use case can be extended to an arbitrary number of satellites. By considering uncertainty quantification techniques such as sensitivity analysis and reliability-based design optimization, this study demonstrates that accounting for uncertainties in the design problem results in a 66% reduction in performance loss compared to the adding of a predefined safety margins, while guaranteeing the feasibility of constraints with high probability.

</details>


### [205] [Decentralized Linearized Consensus ADMM with Efficient Quantized Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19074)
*Boyu Han,Xu Du,Karl H. Johansson,Apostolos I. Rikos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合非精确共识ADMM与有限时间去中心化量化通信的分布式优化算法，能在有向图上仅用量化信息实现全局线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化相比集中式方法在可扩展性和鲁棒性上具有优势，但现有方法存在依赖精确通信、无向图或精确子问题求解等局限，需要更高效的实用算法。

Method: 将非精确共识ADMM（IC-ADMM）与有限时间去中心化量化通信算法相结合，允许在有向通信图上仅交换量化局部信息，且无需精确求解局部子问题。

Result: 在局部目标函数强凸且L-光滑的假设下，算法保证全局线性收敛到最优解邻域；数值实验表明其在收敛速度和通信效率上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提算法兼具有向图支持、量化通信和子问题非精确求解三大优势，是实用高效的分布式优化方案。

Abstract: Distributed optimization offers significant advantages over centralized methods in terms of scalability and robustness when solving large-scale problems. In this paper, we propose a novel decentralized optimization algorithm that integrates Inexact Consensus ADMM (IC-ADMM) with a finite-time decentralized quantized communication algorithm. The proposed method enjoys three main benefits: (i) it operates on directed communication graphs, (ii) it requires only quantized local information instead of exact values, and (iii) it does not rely on solving the local subproblems exactly. Under the assumption that each node's local objective is strongly convex and L-smooth, the algorithm is guaranteed to achieve global linear convergence to a neighborhood of the optimal solution. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate its advantages over existing methods in terms of convergence speed and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [206] [From Operations to Elderly Care Outcomes: A Thematic Review of Industrial Engineering and Decision-Support Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19075)
*Shayan Farhang Pazhooh,Fereshteh Parvaresh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了30篇运筹学与老年护理交叉领域的研究，将其分为居家医疗运营、多药管理与临床时间疗法三大主题。分析显示研究方法从静态确定性模型向动态随机模型及AI集成演变，但存在关键转化鸿沟：当前文献过度聚焦过程优化（如人员排班），难以将运营效率转化为临床结果，且缺乏医院-社区过渡的整体模型。研究提出一个概念框架，推动从孤立运营任务向集成多层级决策转变，强调通过数字孪生、大语言模型等技术实现智慧化护理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球老龄化人口快速增长给医疗系统带来了严峻挑战，需要高效、公平且以患者为中心的护理模式。尽管工业工程与运筹学提供了优化与决策支持工具，但当前应用往往碎片化，缺乏系统整合。

Method: 采用主题综述方法，对30篇运筹学与老年护理交叉领域的代表性研究进行系统分类与分析，归纳为居家医疗运营、多药管理和临床时间疗法三个主题，并追溯方法论的演化轨迹。

Result: 研究发现方法论从静态确定性模型向动态随机模型及AI集成显著演变，但存在关键转化鸿沟：文献过度集中于过程级优化（如人员路由），难以将运营效率转化为可测量的临床结果；医院-社区护理过渡的整体模型严重缺乏。

Conclusion: 提出一个概念框架，将研究焦点从孤立运营任务转向集成多层级决策，强调需要鲁棒系统分析、人本设计和通过数字孪生与大语言模型等新兴数字技术实现护理智慧化，以弥合理论运营指标与患者健康结果之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The rapid growth of the global aging population presents severe challenges to healthcare systems, necessitating efficient, equitable, and patient-centered care models. While Industrial Engineering and Operations Research (OR) provide robust optimization and decision-support tools to address these multidimensional complexities, current applications often remain fragmented. This paper presents a thematic review of 30 seminal studies at the intersection of OR and elderly care, categorizing the literature into home healthcare operations, polypharmacy management, and clinical chronotherapy. Our analysis highlights a significant methodological evolution from static, deterministic models toward dynamic and stochastic frameworks integrated with artificial intelligence (AI). Despite these advancements, a critical translational gap persists: the current OR literature is heavily dominated by process-level optimizations, such as staff routing, and struggles to translate these operational efficiencies into measurable clinical outcomes. Furthermore, holistic models bridging the transition between hospital and community care remain critically underexplored. To develop resilient and smart healthcare systems, this study proposes a conceptual framework that shifts the research focus from isolated operational tasks to integrated, multi-level decision-making. We emphasize the critical need for robust systems analysis, human-inclusive design, and the smartification of care through emerging digital technologies - including digital twins and large language models - to successfully bridge the gap between theoretical operational metrics and tangible patient-level health outcomes.

</details>


### [207] [Active Disturbance Rejection for Boundary Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19144)
*Jukka-Pekka Humaloja,Lassi Paunonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有未知输入扰动和未建模非线性的抽象边界控制系统及受控偏微分方程的镇定问题，采用自抗扰控制方法设计控制器，实现了共位输入输出边界控制系统的扰动抑制、稳定性与外部适定性，并将结果应用于一维波动方程和热方程的控制器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边界控制方法难以处理系统中存在的未知输入扰动和未建模非线性，需要一种能够在不依赖精确扰动模型的情况下实现系统镇定和扰动抑制的控制策略。

Method: 采用自抗扰控制（ADRC）方法，针对共位输入输出（collocated inputs and outputs）的边界控制系统设计控制器，通过扩张状态观测器估计并补偿未知输入扰动，实现闭环系统的稳定性和外部适定性。

Result: 所设计的控制器成功抑制了输入扰动，并保证了闭环系统的稳定性和外部适定性；该方法适用于一类共位输入输出的边界控制系统。

Conclusion: 自抗扰控制方法可有效处理抽象边界控制系统中的未知输入扰动和非线性问题，为一维波动方程和热方程等典型PDE系统提供了可行的控制方案。

Abstract: We consider stabilisation of abstract boundary control systems and controlled partial differential equations with general unknown input disturbances and unmodeled nonlinearities at the input. We utilise the active disturbance rejection control approach to design a controller which rejects the input disturbance and achieves stability and external well-posedness of the closed-loop system for a class of boundary control systems with collocated inputs and outputs. We apply our main results to design controllers for one-dimensional wave and heat equations.

</details>


### [208] [A relaxed-inertial proximal point algorithm for strongly quasiconvex equilibrium problems on Hadamard manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19164)
*Luisa Marie Després,Nicholas Pischke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合惯性步和过松弛技术的邻近点方法，用于求解Hadamard流形上由伪单调和强拟凸双函数生成的均衡问题，并给出了显式、快速且一致的收敛速率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限维欧氏空间上的惯性-过松弛邻近点方法首次推广到Hadamard流形这一非线性设置中，并利用定量分析框架提供更精细的收敛性分析。

Method: 在标准的邻近步基础上引入惯性步和过松弛技术，其中过松弛步骤首次在Hadamard流形框架下处理；采用作者先前工作中发展的强拟凸优化的定量分析方法来建立收敛性。

Result: 获得了迭代点到解的距离的显式、快速且高度一致的收敛速率，这些定量估计在欧氏情形下也具有新意；同时弱化或取消了某些先前假设。

Conclusion: 该工作将Grad、Lara和Marcavillaca在有限维欧氏空间上的方法首次扩展到非线性流形环境，并为均衡问题的邻近点型方法提供了更精细的理论保证。

Abstract: We study a proximal point type method for approximating solutions to equilibrium problems generated by pseudomonotone and strongly quasiconvex bifunctions over Hadamard manifolds, that is complete simply connected Riemannian manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature. Next to the usual proximal step, the method we consider also incorporates an inertia step together with a subsequent over-relaxation, the latter of which is treated in the context of Hadamard manifolds, to our knowledge, for the first time. Making use of a quantitative approach towards such proximal methods for strongly quasiconvex optimization developed by the authors in previous work, we in particular provide effective arguments for the convergence of the method, yielding explicit, fast and very uniform rates of convergence for the distance of the iterates towards the solution. These results extend previous work by Grad, Lara and Marcavillaca on such a method over finite-dimensional Euclidean spaces for the first time to a nonlinear setting, with the quantitative estimates already being novel in the Euclidean case. In particular, our effective approach allows for a fine-grained view on the assumptions on the surrounding objects, so that we are able to either weaken or even fully discharge some previous assumptions.

</details>


### [209] [A Complete Characterization of Optimal Subgradient Methods for Lipschitz Convex Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19240)
*Aaron Zoll,Benjamin Grimmer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对M-Lipschitz凸优化问题，在给定初始距离D的条件下，完整刻画了所有最优固定步长一阶方法，证明这些方法均可通过构造性方法推导得出，并通过证明乘子给出了最优方法的多面体表示，进而证明不存在任意时刻最优的固定步长次梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究识别出若干种由步长矩阵W参数化的最优固定步长方法，但缺乏对所有最优方法的完整刻画。本文旨在给出这种完整刻画，并探究这些方法之间的内在联系和性质。

Method: 通过构造性方法框架，利用证明乘子（proof multipliers）给出所有最优固定步长方法的多面体表示，实现对最优方法集合的完整表征。

Result: (1) 完整刻画了所有信息论最优的固定步长一阶方法；(2) 证明所有最优方法均可由构造性方法导出；(3) 给出了最优方法集合的多面体表示；(4) 证明了不存在任意时刻最优的固定步长次梯度方法。

Conclusion: 固定步长方法中，若要求算法在每一步迭代中都达到最优（anytime optimal），则不存在这样的次梯度方法，这表明固定步长方法在收敛性能上存在内在局限性。

Abstract: We consider the design of optimal fixed-step first-order methods for $M$-Lipschitz convex optimization given $\|x_0-x_\star\|\leq D$. Prior works have identified several distinct fixed-step methods, parameterized by a matrix of stepsizes $W$, with the (information-theoretic) minimax optimal rate $MD/\sqrt{N+1}$ of objective gap convergence. We provide a complete characterization of every optimal fixed-step method. Moreover, we show every optimal fixed-step method can be derived from the constructive approach of~\cite{constructive_approach} and provide a polyhedral representation of the set of optimal methods through proof multipliers. From this characterization, we show that no anytime optimal fixed-step subgradient methods exist.

</details>
